[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-18 · RULINGS-ROUND2 §E (the owner's doctrines of 2026-10-18, verbatim on the BOARD). What each means for THE SITES — every clause already true of the design, now stated. MACRO OVER MICRO ("We're keeping a lot of things at the macro level, not the micro in accordance with what matters which is what happens for the dungeon master and the table."): a site is ONE record whose effects ride numbers the world already keeps — no garrison roll-call, no per-soldier upkeep, no skirmish ledger at a den. MEDIEVAL FOR MILLENNIA ("We're keeping this strictly to the medieval and in the fantasy setting these last for millennia's on end."): the ten kinds are the whole catalog over any span; no era upgrades a site; quality only wears and is restored. NO ENGINE MORTALITY CLOCK ("in fantasy settings, there are longed lived or immortal species and characters. this allows the DM to decide"): a person holder (a wizard, a paragon) leaves a site only by the DM's verdict on that person (§6.1 step 1), never by an age. PURPOSE ONLY BY THE DM; MENACES NEVER EMERGENT: ST15 and ST16. THE SEA AND THE SEASONS ARE IN THE BOOKS AND IN THE CODE (the owner, 06:35: "Regarding weather and Sea route isn't this already in the books?"): the harbour works and the crossing sit ON the landed carriers — M8's lanes and `isPort`, D-6's sea hazards, W-NAVY's navies and blockade, SEASONS-B's slow-not-sever travel law — never beside them (§3.3).]

## THE ANSWER, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

A SITE is a place on the land outside a town — a keep, a watchtower, a mage's tower, a shrine, an inn on the road, a bridge, a mine, a hospice, a harbour mole, a barrow. The DM places it (or adopts one the map offers) and names it; the engine never invents one. Whoever HOLDS it decides what it does: a town's paid garrison in a sound keep makes the towns near it safer, harder to besiege and quicker to hear trouble; the same keep held by brigands or a DM's menace makes every town near it less safe; a small flock of some god that keeps a shrine or a hermitage helps the towns that welcome its god and touches no town that shuts it out — never a hazard; an EMPTY site does nothing at all. Every benefit is bought with upkeep and shrinks with distance and neglect; every hazard grows with the strength of whoever squats there. Sites change hands the way towns do — abandoned when the money runs out, traded with their land, ceded in a peace, granted as a fief, taken by siege, cleared by a sortie or by the party — and the last town that cared for a site gets the first chance to take it back. A site outlives the town built over it. Nothing a site does is a second number: each effect is one bounded lean inside a quantity the world already keeps (the town's defence in a siege, the danger on its roads, the crime and raider pressures, the plague's care, a creed's standing, a route's cost, what a town has heard), so every decider that already reads those quantities — the war chooser, the siege, the caravans, the migrants, the passage planner, the faith contest, the plague — considers sites with no wiring of its own.

| `worldPulse/pulseKernel.js` stage order (frozen 1581) | "`const rumors = advanceRumorLedgers({`" (~2097) before "`const calamity = advanceCalamity({`" (~2527) | a telling written in ST's stage (in `advanceCalamity`'s head) is seeded into the rumor ledgers the NEXT tick — the measured reason the at-hand sighting is a WRITTEN BELIEF ROW in the holder's own belief record (MN's, THE BODIES'), never a telling and never a row on ST's record (§2.4; A.17 · A.5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5: re-read at master, `D-ST-r5-probe/master-reads-r5.out` m1] | — | 1581 (ZERO lines) | — |
| `worldPulse/warCapacityReads.js :: coalitionJoinFeasibility` | "`defenderCurrent: defender.homeDefense,`" (called from the frozen `applyWorldPulse.js`) | one more reader of `homeDefense` that sees the keep by construction (§7.1's census; the walker lists it) | `warLayerEnabled` | 143 | derived |

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · L-BD 30 · A.17 · note 1: four substrate rows added, each read at master `81f1cd963` (`D-ST-r4-probe/measure-1.out`, `measure-2.out`, `measure-3.out`).]

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-3 · HOST-r2-20 · ST-r2-12 · HOST-r2-9 · HOST-r2-7: four substrate rows added, each read at master `81f1cd963`.]

**The binding prior words.** The owner's 10-17 doctrines (the tool is the DM's — "We only supply setting and consequence"; BEHIND THE SCENES; UNPREDICTABLE IN PROSPECT, LEGIBLE AND COHERENT ON SCRUTINY; HEARINGS ONLY ON THE WEEK); the hosting doctrine (above); the Evidence Law; THE PROMISE (a seed is a starting world forever — no site is ever generated into a seed); MN12 and the owner's ninth word (menaces are the DM's alone); the map builder's standing rules (`refs/HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18.md` "Encounter maps and world sites": law 1 "A site's status change or an adoption must reach the host as a dated event", law 3 "No absolute week numbers until the real scale is written", law 10 "No invented fact", J384 (10) "Holy places and hidden cult sites are only DM-placed, tagged with a deity, DM-only by default, and offered to the host for adoption. Never generated." (the host's "cult" is a small flock's holy place — the faith ladder's `cult` standing, never villainy: A.3, the owner's "not all cults are evil"), J384 (11) steadings are members of the parent's realm, not sites, ENC-HP-01 a settlement founded on a site adopts it); the fold §3.1.28 (the world-site record is THE SITES') and §3.1.29 (garrisons and held sites are THE SITES'; the WC inbox's vassal-garrison row moves here; OD-PL-33); THE BODIES (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_BD.md`, compiled 2026-10-18 on the owner's six messages of 18:1x–19:3x): a body is the sixth actor kind (RULINGS-ROUND3 §G.3), its record the one truth of its might, supplies and stance; its routes to ST (BD §19) are cured here in round 4 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes].

**The population facts.** No site exists in any seed or save today (`rg -w worldSites` over master: 0 files). The digest carries per-cell territory and tier-1/2/3 neighbours on canonized worlds; aspatial worlds carry only the relationship graph. `treasuryEnabled` is lit nowhere; `warLayerEnabled` only in the war presets; the rumor road only under infoMode `full`.

---

## §1 THE LAWS (ST1–ST20)

**ST1 — A SITE IS ON THE LAND; AN INSTITUTION IS IN THE TOWN.** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-9: ST owns SITE garrisons only — a held site's garrison band is `garrisonBandOf` (§2.4; since round 3 a READINESS word on `SITE_READINESS`, never an army band — A.10); a SEAT's force band and garrison presence (H13's `PolitySeat.military.forceBand` and `.garrisonPresent`: "no garrison or standing band exists today, so it must be created") are not sites, and their owner is a slot for the war volume's capacity facets (J384 (4)'s measure) — the fold §3.1.29's "garrisons and held sites are THE SITES'" is routed to narrow to SITE garrisons.] A site is a place outside a settlement with a kind, a name and a holder. It is never an institution: `institutionFounding.js` and the institution roster are untouched, and a town's own walls stay its institutions (already folded into its generated military score — `defenseLedger.js`'s header names the double count it cured). No second institution system.

**ST2 — ONE SITE TRUTH, ONE WRITER.** `spatialLedgers.worldSites` is the only HOST record of a site; it is the host half of the map's asked `WorldSiteHost`/`WorldSiteRecord` (ENC-HL-01..03). The map keeps its OWN site document for its own values ("Every other site value is the map's own", ENC-MS-01) and JOINS this record by the map's site id (`mapSiteRef`, §2.1), so the HOST facts — existence, kind, name, holder, status, position — have one truth, never two; the channels are ST-4's (DR-V14-1 for the record, H13's `PolitySite` for held sites, H9's events window for each dated status) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-7: "the map reads it through its window and never keeps a second" was false of the map's own design]. One module writes it — `src/domain/sites/siteLedger.js :: applySiteEvent(worldState, event) → { worldState, siteId, applied }` — called only by a CLOSED, pinned list of callers (the realm verb arm, ST's one stage, the war layer's keep leaf, PT's party door for `party_cleared_site` (ST-r2-19), the existing writers of §6.3's roads, and THE BODIES' ledger and stage [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (4) · A.16] — a walker fails on any other importer); every write is a dated EVENT (law 1 of the host). [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5: THE PATROLS' sighting input is STRUCK from the closed list — a patrol's look writes into the holder's own belief record by its owner's door (MN's, THE BODIES'), never through ST's writer; ST's record holds only the TRUTH of the holder, and the one writer RETURNS the sightings an event occasions for its caller to hand to the holder's belief owner (§2.4).] Drop-when-empty at every optional key and at the ledger; absent while dark.

**ST3 — THE ENGINE NEVER SPAWNS A SITE.** The DM PLACES a site, or ADOPTS one the map offers (map-tier until adopted; the adoption is the DM's act), or accepts "leave a ruin" on a dying settlement's card. The engine changes a site's FATE (who holds it, its quality, its state) and never its existence. Ids are minted zero-rng (FNV over the minting verb's event id — RN's deed-id idiom), never borrowed.

**ST4 — NO SITE DECIDES ANYTHING; HOLDERS DECIDE.** A site has no mind, no economy, no faction, no belief, no population. Its holder's own deciders act (a court funds or abandons; a menace's course is the DM's; a body — gang, beast or conclave — takes, keeps and releases a site by its own weekly choice, THE BODIES §4 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · BD's route: "a brigand body moves by WC-12's rules" → WC-12's located raiders are THE BODIES' gang records, BD §19]); the site only changes the numbers those deciders already read.

**ST5 — EVERY EFFECT IS ONE BOUNDED LEAN INSIDE A QUANTITY THE WORLD ALREADY KEEPS.** Defence (`homeDefense`), danger (the one presence term), crime and raider pressure (the gates and counterforces), readiness (the threat index), health (`care01Of`), faith (the contest's strength), magic (the arcane plane), route cost (`scoreRoute`), and what a town notices (THE PATROLS' `witnessedCrossing`, through ST's registered input). Each lean is a named `{ mult, reason }` row or a named additive term at one seam, clamped, DRAFT, UNMEASURED, on the FP-46 roster. No site has a number of its own that any decider reads; no second defence score, danger, crime, plague, faith or magic law.

**ST6 — FUNDING IS A DERIVED BAND, NEVER A COIN STORE; THE DM'S BAND IS A CEILING.** [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's "the ONE upkeep-shaped sink (the `UPKEEP_LEDGERS` row the champions volume uses)" does not exist: `UPKEEP_LEDGERS = ['deployments']`, `advanceTreasury(settlement, options = {})` takes no worldState and is called once from the frozen kernel.] A holder's DERIVED band is read each tick from what it has: a settlement from its prosperity label (`settlementFundingBand`, §2.4 — THE ONE settlement band, which THE PATROLS' pay gate reads too), a creed from its standing in the holder's seat town, a person from its purse band (CH's derived purse), a BODY (a gang, or a conclave holding as `faith`) from its own supplies and never above `meagre` — THE REPAIR CEILING, lifted for a conclave only by a patron court that endows its creed (§2.4; L-BD 31) — and a menace or a beast from nothing (its sites only wear) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — L-BD 31]. The DM may set a band by hand (`fundingSet`), and it is a CEILING: the EFFECTIVE band is `min(fundingSet, derived)` on the order `unfunded < meagre < funded < lavish`, so a holder whose prosperity falls lets its sites go even under a DM's band — the owner's "these areas can also simply be abandonded due to lower prosperity" fires on every world now, before any treasury lights — and the DM's band can only hold a site LOWER than the holder's means (a lord who stops paying). THE DM'S LEVERS ON A POOR HOLDER'S KEEP ARE TWO, both through the record and neither a site override: the HOLDER — `SITE_HOLDER` hands the keep to a holder whose means pay for it (a richer court, a crown, a creed's seat) — and the HOLDER'S OWN MEANS, which are the settlement's prosperity and move only where the settlement's own economy moves (the landed upswing mover steps it, `upswingKernel.js`: "`economicState: { ...ec, prosperity: nextProsperity }`"; a DM act on the settlement where one exists — PLAUSIBLE, no landed edit of `economicState.prosperity` was found at master, `cure/D-ST-r3.md` Q1). Re-garrisoning a keep with a holder that is still `unfunded` buys it ONE season, never more: every holder change drops the want's clock (`wantSince`, §6.1 step 3) and the clock re-arms on the next unfunded read, so the new garrison walks away a season later, in the open, with its receipt; and EVERY HOLDER CHANGE DROPS THE DM'S CEILING (`fundingSet`) WITH THE CLOCK — the holder compared as its FULL IDENTITY (class · ref · role · creed, §2.1) — so a new holder starts from its own derived band and the DM's first lever works the tick it is pulled (a keep a lord stopped paying for, handed to `Prosperous` Ashford, is `lavish` at once; S3b) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.20 · ST-r2-16 (a)(b): the r3 cure compared `holder.ref` alone, so Kell's garrison → Kell's clergy kept the stale clock, and never dropped `fundingSet`, so the "lord who stops paying" ceiling (`unfunded`) outlived the lord and the new holder abandoned a season later]. A BODY holder's want is its own supplies: it keeps or leaves a site by its own weekly choice (THE BODIES §4, through ST's writer), never by this clock (§6.1 step 3). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-16 (RULINGS-ROUND3 B): round 2's "to keep a poor town's keep manned regardless, the DM re-garrisons it by `SITE_HOLDER`" was false — the stale clock abandoned it again at once, and even a fresh clock cannot lift `min(fundingSet, derived)` above the holder's means; "manned regardless" is STRUCK]. COIN is the W-COIN slot (RULINGS-ROUND2 D): ONE upkeep law (`treasury.js :: computeUpkeep`, "ONE LAW PARAMETERIZED BY LEDGER"), widened by `UPKEEP_LEDGERS` members each with its caller in the same act — W-SEAT SEAT-8's `civilContests` · `interventions` (their charters) and FP's `sites` · `retainers` (CH) · `patrols` (PR): one roster, one law. FP's three ride ONE leaf-first car, because the frozen kernel's only call passes no worldState ("`advanceTreasury(sieged, { interval: tickInterval, tick: worldState.tick, deployment, blockade, rules: simulationRules })`"): the holder's roll is carried on the settlement record `advanceTreasury` already receives, written by the owning stage through its `settlementUpdates` and read the next tick (CH OD-CH-10's car; PLAUSIBLE, measured at that unit) — never a leaf inside `treasury.js` reading worldState, never a kernel line. When it lands, the derived band reads the PAID share. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-1 (RULINGS-ROUND2b: the DM's band a ceiling over the prosperity-derived band; the rows the W-COIN slot with a named car) · SEAMS-r2-12 (COIN separated from the BAND; the settlement-keyed band exported for the owner's patrol word "also size and number of patrols gated by tier, population, and prosperity and internal secruity").]

**ST7 — EMPTY IS NOTHING.** A site with no holder leans on nothing — no bonus, no hazard, no draw (the owner's seventh word). Its quality still wears, so a later holder inherits what neglect left. For an EMPTY site, a call-board entry, a dungeon's draw for adventurers or a "something stirs in the old barrow" line is the DM's to author, never automatic; a HELD den is not empty — its call is the attached court's need on THE CHAMPIONS' board (§7.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-26].

**ST8 — THE UNTIED HOLDER IS A GENERAL HAZARD.** A site held by a force tied to no settlement — a GANG or a BEAST (THE BODIES' engine-born bodies), a DM's MENACE, a villain paragon — gives NO town a bonus and EVERY attached town a hazard, scaled by the holder's MIGHT × the site's quality × the distance band (the owner's second word: "noone gets bonuses but everyone gets varying degrees of negative"). A CONCLAVE is never an untied holder and its SITE is never a hazard: a creed's flock holding a site holds it as `faith` (§2.1; §3.1 says how its sign follows each town's stance toward the creed); a HOSTILE flock's harm — a creed a town curbs or outlaws may raid that town for supplies and bodies and take captives (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.2, the owner's words) — is its own ACTS through THE BODIES' rows, never a term of its site [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2's ST-X2 · B.2: "never a hazard" narrowed to the site, so it no longer reads as a claim about the flock's own raids] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · SEAMS-r3-1 (BLOCKING) · ST-W1: "a brigand body, a cult" STRUCK — the owner, BOARD ~17:27: "remember that not all cults are evil. cults are simply small conclaves of religion"; ~18:5x: "a conclave with a good natured diety might have welcome reception and not be a public enemy"]. Danger enters as ONE registered feeder of MN's `presenceTerms` — MN §3.3's ONE COMPOSITION SENTENCE, which this volume cites and never restates (§3.4), built as the SHARED CAR of A.3 — so three dens on one road never triple the danger (requirement 2). A HELD suppressor site never lowers the war's own drivers: it SUBTRACTS only from the suppressible part (MN's `roadCountry`), floored at zero, inside the one σ shared with THE PATROLS under one 0.25 cap, and NEVER from the walls term or an enemy column (OD-PS-19 STANDS: an army in the land always latches), so a besieged town never falls out of the latch (`W_SIEGE 0.85 − SECURITY_MAX_RELIEF 0.25 − ENTER_THRESHOLD 0.55` leaves 0.05 of margin). A KEPT ROAD keeps a calm town calm where the suppressed raid part stays at or below the latch — the counterforce working, by design (RULINGS-ROUND2b; MN M50) — but it never ends an embattlement the raid still feeds: the landed step lets an embattled town go only "`if (rawT < T.EXIT_THRESHOLD && (now - priorSince) >= T.MIN_DWELL_TICKS)`" (`EXIT_THRESHOLD` 0.30), so a menace's raid or a `capable` den kept down to 0.35 holds it embattled for as long as it presses, its level easing toward 0.35, while a `modest` den kept down to 0.15 lets a town with a low ramp of its own (T₀ below about 0.18) go after the dwell (EXECUTED, `cure/D-ST-r3-probe/latchExit.out`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-8 (the composer is MN's; ST registers) · RULINGS-ROUND2b (L-ST item 4's "never below the latch's floor" STRUCK)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — SEAMS-r2-7 (ST's mirror of MN5's "may fall out", which the MN verifier executed false for a raid) · MN-V6 (A.3: the composer is a shared car) · note 3 (cite, never restate)]. The latch also tells what each hazard IS: only an untied FORTIFICATION at hand (a `capable` gang or stronger in a sound keep) crosses `ENTER_THRESHOLD` alone; every other untied kind's feeder is a TIPPER AND A SUSTAINER — it latches a town already pressed by crime, exhaustion or a neighbour's war (`T₀ + p·(1 − T₀) > 0.55`), keeps an embattled town embattled, and otherwise moves no danger consumer — while its OTHER arms fire regardless of the latch: the gate rows and the counterforce on every attached town by its distance band, the readiness membership on the AT-HAND towns only (ST9 governs) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 7: round 2 said the membership fired "on every attached town at every band"]; its felt lines say only what those arms did (§14) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-9: the landed step latches a calm record only "`if (rawT > T.ENTER_THRESHOLD)`"; each kind tabled in `cure/D-ST-r2-probe/scaleAndLatch.out`]. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's "danger on the roads under it lowered within bounds" made exact: the only lowering is on what dens, menaces and roosts added; patrols (THE PATROLS, `briefs/L-PR.md` item 5) subtract on the same term.]

**ST9 — A FORT IS NOT A THREAT; A DEN IS.** The readiness index (`buildThreatByCid`) counts an untied site at hand by MEMBERSHIP: its at-hand towns join the index's `raidHot` set ("all reads are membership tests"), and a menace holder's towns are already there by MN's own membership row, so nothing counts twice — the town mobilizes against the den on its road [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-13: the compile's weighted 0.4 input had no seam in a membership index and double-counted a menace holder]. THE VEIL HOLDS HERE TOO: an at-hand town joins `raidHot` only for an untied site that is NOT `keptSecret` (or has been `made_known`) — a den whose arrival was TOLD (§2.4) — never for a veiled one [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-15 (WC-ST-31; RULINGS-P5 C.15): the membership read the den's TRUTH with no veil, so a kept-secret den mobilized its at-hand towns — a readiness that leaked a den no court had heard of (A.16: "NO telling of it until `made_known`"); the threat index takes no belief selection, so the selection moves to the record's PUBLIC state — ITS READ CLASS, named once (CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)): the PUBLIC record; a court that WITNESSED a kept-secret den answers it through its own belief read at the clearing fork (§6.1 step 6), never through this index]. A held fort adds no readiness term: it is standing defence, not alarm. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's "READINESS ↑" for a held fort would have RAISED mobilization through a threat index whose sign is alarm; the fort's gain is defence, security and notice, its den's cost is alarm.]

**ST10 — THE READY KEEP IS THE TOWN'S FIRST LINE.** A `fortification` at hand that is `sound` and held by a funded settlement garrison enters `homeDefense` as a LARGE bounded term (the owner's fifth word: "drastically"), named on the envelope (`siteDefense` = `KEEP_DEFENSE_MAX` 18 × the keep's ONE readiness value `siteReadiness01`, §2.4). The term is the keep's DEFENSIBILITY — walls, height and stores, manned by the SITE'S OWN COMPLEMENT: a standing garrison paid through the holder by the site's funding band, drawn from NO town's manpower pool and never from its riders (macro over micro: no roll-call, no per-soldier upkeep). The keep neither marches nor is drawn — the levy draws riders, never a keep. The men who ride the keep's stretches are THE PATROLS' riders, from the town's one pool (PR3), and PR's round walks a held keep's stretches first; they are DIFFERENT MEN, so the keep's road rows (§3.3) and the riders' rows are two feeders of ONE capped presence suppression (`min(0.25, σ_site + σ_patrol)`) and ONE counterforce composition — never a double count, the cap the runaway guard [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 · PR-V2 · SEAMS-r2-9 (A.1, which corrects the chair's L-PR item 3): round 2's "never a second body of men … the keep's suppression IS its holder's riders" is STRUCK — the garrison is the site's]. The siege verdict reads it on BOTH cores (§4.1): the aspatial capacity roll directly; on every canonized world the feasibility gate, the ceiling and capitulation through `defenderCurrent`, plus a LONGER starvation clock — and NEVER through `relief01`, so a bigger keep never raises pFall (a pin, S19). MN-2's menace verdict reads it through `homeDefense`; allied relief subtracts it (a keep never marches to another town's walls). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-4 (RULINGS-ROUND2b: both arms, never `relief01`; executed, `cure/D-ST-r2-probe/siegeMonotone.out`) · SEAMS-r2-9 (one body of men; the keep adds defensibility) — ↳ r3: "one body of men" superseded by A.1 above.] The dossier's defence page reads the same leaf (one term, two readers).

**ST11 — UNSCATHED IS A STOCK THE CRISIS SPENDS FIRST.** In its function's crisis a ready site is spent before the town: a siege's ROLLED fall lands on the keep first (`sound → worn`, then `worn → ruined`) and only then on the town — never a fall the DM gave (a hearing seal or an HBF-86 pin), a capitulation or the `SIEGE_MAX_AGE` storm (§4.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-5]; a plague overwhelms the hospice before the town's own care; a desecration strikes the shrine before the seated creed. Each spend is a dated event with its receipt. A relieved or lifted crisis leaves the site as it stands; funding restores it over years (§4.3).

**ST12 — EVERY POSITIVE NAMES ITS COUNTERFORCE.** Upkeep (the funding band's wear), resentment (a crown's garrison in a member's land — PL's control instrument, written as PL's `riders_quartered` row, its garrison arm (iii), PL §4.7 F.6 (b)), the siege it invites (a fort is a target), a traitor at the gate (ES's product — a fold §3.1 ES INBOX row; DARK until ES carries it), a den if the garrison is withdrawn [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-42 · WC-ST-43; RULINGS-P5 C.17): each volume pointed at the other — the garrison's word is PL's `riders_quartered` (PL's lane carried it, P58) and the traitor is ROUTED to the fold's ES row]. A den invites a clearing. Requirement 2, per kind (§3).

**ST13 — THE LAST TO ACT HAS THE FIRST CLAIM; NOTHING IS FORBIDDEN.** The record keeps `lastHolder` (the last settlement-tied holder) and the week it last ACTED — a DATED act only: `garrisoned`, `restored`, `reclaimed`, or the DM's funding set on its site (a passively derived band writes no event and is no act) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-16]. When a site falls empty or is taken by an untied force, that court is OFFERED THE RECLAIM first, in the first week it BELIEVES the site so (`site_reclaim`; §6.1 step 5) — a den it has not heard of or seen is never reclaimed [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.4 · the ruling's ST-X2 (V1): the offer was minted on truth]; a decline, or a court that no longer stands, opens it to whoever acts next. A preference, never a right (the owner's second word).

**ST14 — A SITE OUTLIVES ITS TOWN.** No settlement's death deletes a site (the owner's third word). When a holder or anchor settlement fails (a remnant status, a razing, an abandonment, a world imported without it), the site PERSISTS: holder → none if it was that settlement, an `abandoned` event, quality unchanged then wearing, its attachments re-derived to the surviving neighbours, and the dead court's first claim LAPSES. A dying settlement's card OFFERS "leave a ruin" — a `ruin` site at its place, the DM's act — with the dead settlement as `lastHolder` for the chronicle only; the card's HOST is named at ST-0's car-0 (§10) [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-64)].

**ST15 — WHO MAKES A DEN.** A den arises only when an UNTIED holder takes a site: an engine-born GANG or BEAST (THE BODIES — emergent, the world's own, born from a fact the world holds, BD2; WC-12's located raiders ARE THE BODIES' gang records, BD §19; until C17 lands, engine-born dens do not occur), or a MENACE — and a menace is DM-AUTHORED ONLY and never organically emergent (MN12; the owner's ninth word): a menace takes a site only where the DM's course sent it. A CONCLAVE makes no den: a flock that takes a site holds it as `faith` — a shrine, a hermitage, a church's keep (§2.1, §3.1). Every lifecycle and scenario row says which dens are emergent and which are the DM's. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · SEAMS-r3-1 · ST-W1: "a cult (the faith system's own …)" STRUCK as a den-maker; BD's route (1).]

**ST16 — PURPOSE AND DESTINY ARE THE DM'S.** A site has no purpose, no quest and no destiny unless the DM directs one; left alone it is consequence only (the owner's ninth word and the hosting doctrine). The party is never made important by a site; a den on the road is a fact of the world, never a call aimed at the party.

**ST17 — WHAT A TOWN HEARS IS BELIEF; THE NOTICING READ IS THE PATROLS'.** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 5: the harbour works' witness input is STRUCK — it added nothing: PS8 makes every harbour landing witnessed ("for a harbour LANDING … `settlement(B)`, where B's own seat is on the path and the crossing is therefore ALWAYS witnessed"), and a harbour's watch is the port's navy (PR15; OD-PR-37: "a harbour's watch is its W-NAVY navy").] A held watchtower is a REGISTERED INPUT to THE PATROLS' one noticing read `witnessedCrossing` (RULINGS-ROUND2 C Q1: "PR OWNS `witnessedCrossing`"; PR10 already lists "a held watchtower stands on the stretch (ST17)"): its holder's power witnesses what crosses the tower's stretch or country, so the passage volume's quiet crossing is defeated by a manned tower, and the report ("Thornwatch's watchers saw the column cross the ford") is that crossing's receipt. ST keeps no witness set of its own — `siteWitnesses` (§2.4) is the input PR reads, nothing else. A watchtower town is never written into a telling's `partyIds`: that field means the INVOLVED settlements (RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §5), and a town that saw from its tower was not involved; a tower's part in the rumor road's arrival records is a slot on IN's witness plane (IN's inbox), never a fake party. Nothing here reveals truth outside the omniscient arm or places a site's id where a settlement's belongs (the hazard MN7 cured). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-11.]

**ST18 — SITES ARE CONSIDERED EVERYWHERE, BY CONSTRUCTION, AND BY ONE READ WHERE A SITE MUST BE KNOWN AS A SITE.** Every effect enters a single read the deciders already consume (§7's COVERAGE CENSUS, pinned by a walker: no decider reads a parallel number); where a decider must know a site AS a site — the war chooser's target, the passage planner's path, the call board, the menace's course, a paragon's choice, the party's doors, the dossier, a hearing's WHERE — it reads ONE derived read, `sitesNear(worldState, settlementId)` / `siteAt(worldState, siteId)` (`siteAt`'s readers, named: the DOSSIER's site page, the hearing card's WHERE, PL's garrison arm `riders_quartered` (iii) — "its holder read off `siteAt`" — and ST's own `closeSiteWeek` [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-13 (WC-ST-1; RULINGS-P5 C.13 · CHAIR-NOTES 20 (b): the dossier is a reader)]); a hearing card names the site as its WHERE (`ENGAGEMENT_WHERE_KINDS`' `site`) and carries it on the `subject` dial. A site is NEVER an actor: it has no mind (ST4), so it is a WHERE and a subject only (§7.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-14 (the RULINGS-ROUND2b ADDENDUM, applied; A.7): round 2's STOP marker here is replaced by the ruling].

**ST19 — THE MOUNT RULE.** Zero lines in `pulseKernel.js` (1581), `applyWorldPulse.js` (907), `warTermination.js` (818); nothing in `armyTransitKernel.js` (798) or `generosityKernel.js` (800). ST's one stage is a leaf after MN's at the head of `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` (461); every lean is a leaf the named host calls in ≤ 4 lines; every host near the ceiling (`stressorDynamics.js` 759) takes its lines through a leaf-first car measured at the wave's car-0.

**ST20 — THE PHYSICAL WORLD IS A SEAM.** Until the world canon carries a site's point, WHERE is the country around a settlement (`{ kind: 'country', ids: [a] }`) or a road between two (`{ kind: 'road', ids: [a, b] }`) — MN's WHERE idiom, node-keyed; distance is the relationship graph's hop count. On a canonized world the attachment bands read the digest's tiers and `hopWeeks` relative to the realm's median primary hop (`calibration`), never absolute weeks (the host's law 3). A site's WORLD POINT comes from its PLACEMENT, never from the canon: the map's placement at `PLACE_SITE` or at adoption (ENC-HL-03: "the record's world position through V13's `Placement` … read by G3 as a fixed placement") sets `where.position` additively, and from then the host-fixed position outranks the map's siting; a site placed after birth never enters the canon (J388's additive re-canonize covers "only the added seats"); never a cell key (HOST-r1-11) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-7 (3)]. Every wave is whole without the map.

## §2 THE ONTOLOGY

### 2.1 The record (persisted shape 1 of 2; OD-ST-1)

```
spatialLedgers.worldSites[siteId] = {
  kind,                 // SITE_KINDS (§2.2)
  name,                 // the DM's words, quoted, never parsed (MN2's law); '' reads "an unnamed <kind word>"
  where: { kind: 'country' | 'road', ids: [a] | [a, b], position? },   // node-keyed now; `position` (a world point) when the canon carries one (ST20)
  holder?: { class, ref, role?, creed?, might? },                       // ABSENT = unheld (ST7); `might` ONLY on an id-less authored gang (§2.1); the TRUTH of the holder only — no sighting, no belief (A.5: a court's sighting is a row of the holder's own belief record, MN's or THE BODIES', §2.4)
  quality,              // SITE_QUALITY: sound · worn · ruined (stepped only by dated events)
  fundingSet?,          // a DM-set FUNDING_BAND — a CEILING: the effective band is min(fundingSet, derived) (ST6); DROPPED by the one writer at EVERY holder change (A.20)
  wantSince?,           // the tick the EFFECTIVE band of a tied holder (never a body, §6.1 step 3) first read `unfunded`; dropped when it reads above AND by the one writer on EVERY holder change (§6.1 step 3)
  lastHolder?: { ref, actedTick },                                      // the last SETTLEMENT-tied holder and the week it last acted (ST13)
  holderSince?,         // the tick the CURRENT holder came to hold it — set by the one writer at EVERY holder change (the full-identity comparison of §6.1 step 3), absent when unheld; read by `holderSinceOf` (§2.4) [P5: WC-ST-93]
  siege?: { by: actorRef, sinceTick },                                  // a standing `site_siege` (§8); `quietWeeks` STRUCK [P5: WC-ST-91]
  keptSecret?: true,    // NO telling of it until `made_known`; until then a court knows it only by WITNESS, for that court alone (A.16, §2.4) — COURT knowledge only; the party's view is the map's reveal list (J384 (10)'s "DM-only by default" is AUDIENCE: the hard-deny + the reveal list)
  deity?,               // a shrine's tag (J384 (10)): the creed key the DM chose
  resourceKey?,         // a `resource` site: the roster entry it adopts by reference (`deriveResourceSites`)
  placedBy,             // 'dm' · 'adopted' · 'ruin_left'
  offerRef?,            // an adopted site: the map's offer id (provenance; ENC-HP-01)
  mapSiteRef?,          // the map's own site id — the JOIN KEY carried both ways (adoption, retirement at a founding, re-emission at a death)
  events: [{ word, tick, causedBy, by?, from? }],                       // SITE_EVENTS, newest last, bounded ring of 8 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED); the map's dated statuses (ENC-HL-02); `from` = the prior kind on `rekinded` only
  sinceTick, lastTick,
}
```

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-16 (`wantSince`) · HOST-r2-8 (`mapSiteRef`, `from`) · HOST-r2-19 (`keptSecret` replaces the compile's `hidden`, whose word was the map's PARTY secrecy state) · ST-r2-1 (`fundingSet` a ceiling): each round-2 field drop-when-absent, declared under OD-ST-1's taking (OD-ST-34).] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-16 (RULINGS-ROUND3 B: `wantSince` RESETS on every holder change — the writer compares `holder.ref` before and after each event and drops the clock whenever it differs: `placed · garrisoned · taken · reclaimed · conveyed · ceded · granted · seized · consecrated · sold · cleared · abandoned`) · ST-V1 (`holder.seenBy?`, the at-hand witnesses of an untied arrival, written once at the arrival and dropped with the holder): declared under OD-ST-1's taking (OD-ST-40).] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.20 · ST-r2-16 (a)(b): the holder comparison is the FULL IDENTITY `{ class, ref.kind, ref.bodyKind, ref.id, role, creed }` (A.20's tuple `{ kind, id, role }` read as the class and the ref's kind both, plus the creed — Kell's garrison handed to Kell's own clergy, or one creed's clergy to another's at the same seat, IS a holder change) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — `creed` and `ref.bodyKind` added to A.20's tuple, so no holder change the ruling names can slip past it], and `fundingSet` is DROPPED at every holder change (a new holder starts from its own derived band) · A.17 · ST-W2: `seenBy` entries carry the might band seen at their tick (`{ id, mightBand, tick }`), never a live read · A.3: `holder.might` only on an id-less authored gang: declared under OD-ST-1's taking (OD-ST-43).] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5 · the ruling's ST-X3 (V1): `holder.seenBy` is WITHDRAWN from the record (rounds 3 and 4 added it; nothing is built, so nothing migrates). On the holder object a sighting's life was the holder's TENURE: when the holder left or ended unseen — a truth event — the court's belief vanished with it (belief tracking truth, Law One's breach), and at a kept-secret site a witnessing court's whole picture went the tick the den did. A sighting is now a row of the holder's OWN belief record, keyed by the court (MN's `believedMenaces`, THE BODIES' `believedBodyOf`), which outlives the holder's departure, stale, until a newer sighting or a telling (§2.4; OD-ST-49).] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-13 (WC-ST-91; RULINGS-P5 C.13: "`siege.quietWeeks` → struck unless ST names its writer"): nothing wrote or read it — a site siege ends by its verdict (`holds · falls · relieved · lifted`, §8) and the besieger's own mover, never a quiet clock (MN's `quietWeeks` is MN's record) — so it is STRUCK; nothing is built, nothing migrates.] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-93; RULINGS-P5 C.17: "ST exports `holderSinceOf` for BD's tenure read"): BD's hamlet end read the holder's tenure from the `events` ring, whose eight-event horizon can evict the event; `holderSince?` is the ONE stored tenure — set by the one writer at every holder change, dropped when the site falls unheld — declared under OD-ST-1's taking (OD-ST-52), read only through `holderSinceOf` (§2.4).]

- **Holder.** `class ∈ HOLDER_CLASSES = settlement · faith · person · untied`. `ref` is the ONE actor-ref convention `{ kind, id? }` (fold §3.3.12): `settlement` → `{ kind: 'settlement', id }` with `role ∈ garrison · civil`; `faith` → the clergy of a creed, `creed` the deity key, served EITHER by a SEAT — `{ kind: 'settlement', id: <the seat whose clergy serve> }` — OR by a CONCLAVE, THE BODIES' moving flock — `{ kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'conclave', id }` (a conclave's site is a church's, never a hazard: BD §2.1); `person` → `{ kind: 'person', id: 'wnpc_…' }` (a ledger person; a paragon's living `polarity` decides its sign, §3.1); `untied` → `{ kind: 'menace', id }` (MN's record — its might is READ through MN, never copied) or `{ kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'gang' | 'beast', id }` (THE BODIES' record, `spatialLedgers.bodies` — its might is READ from it, never copied; a lair is a site held by a beast, no new kind). BEFORE THE BODIES LAND (C15 precedes C17) a DM-AUTHORED gang is `{ kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'gang' }` with NO `id` and the DM's `might` on `holder.might` — the ONLY holder whose might ST stores — and BD-0's adoption (BD §3.2 (b) `den_held`) mints its body record, fills the `id` and drops `holder.might` through ST's one writer, so from then the record's might is the one truth. A COURT TAKES A SITE AS ITS KIND WANTS: whenever a court comes to hold a site by an engine road — a sale (`sold`), a won clearing or seizure (`cleared`, §6.1 step 6d), a reclaim — a `shrine` passes to its seat's CLERGY (`faith`, the seat's seated creed: a holy place serves a creed, never a garrison) and every other kind to its GARRISON (`settlement`, role `garrison`) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 3: `sold` handed every demanded site "to the court's garrison", so a shrine the court bought read no creed on its own row (§3.3 shrine: "the holding creed's local strength")]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · SEAMS-r3-1 (BLOCKING) · ST-W1 · BD's route (1) and (4): the compile's `{ kind: 'band', id? }` and `{ kind: 'cult', id? }` are STRUCK as holder kinds — `band` is the landed SIZE word (708 src files; four of this volume's own vocabularies are "bands") and `cult` is the faith ladder's landed STANDING (`religionState.js :: standingFor`: "`… (share >= E ? 'established' : 'cult')`"), and the owner's "remember that not all cults are evil. cults are simply small conclaves of religion" (BOARD ~17:27) forbids a cult read as a hazard; in this volume `cult` now means the faith standing only (§2.4) — `D-ST-r4-probe/census-r4.out`.] THE ACTOR CONVENTION IS THE SIX KINDS `settlement · person · party · menace · corps · body` (A.12; RULINGS-ROUND3 §G.3, `bodyKind` a field): a body DECIDES (its weekly choice is its own, BD §4), so on a hearing card a body holder or arriver is an actor `{ kind: 'body', id }`; the convention gains no `site` (a WHERE and a subject, never an actor — it has no mind, ST4); an id-less authored gang has no record and no mind of its own, so it rides the `subject` dial as its holder ref until BD-0 adopts it. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — `band` and `cult` holder REF kinds — ↳ r4: WITHDRAWN by A.3.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-14 (the RULINGS-ROUND2b ADDENDUM, applied; A.7): the compile's "the convention widens by `site` alone" is WITHDRAWN; `site` is a WHERE and a subject, never an actor — ↳ r4: A.7's five kinds amended to six by A.12; "a band or a cult … until WC-12 mints a band actor" superseded by THE BODIES' `body` actor.]
- **State is derived, never stored.** `held` when `holder` is present, else `unheld`. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's STATE `held · unheld · ruined` put `ruined` on one record twice (a state and a quality); `ruined` is the QUALITY band alone (one word, one meaning per record) and "a ruin" in prose is a site of quality `ruined`.]
- **Attachments are derived, never stored.** [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's persisted `attachedTo: [{ settlementId, distanceBand }]` would go stale the week a neighbour dies; derived, it RE-POINTS by construction (the owner's third word, ST14).] `attachmentsOf(worldState, siteId, digest?) → [{ settlementId, band }]`, codepoint-sorted, dead settlements skipped (`lifecycleStatusOf` non-empty or absent from the campaign): the anchor (`ids[0]`; both ends of a road) at `at_hand`; on a canonized world tier-1 neighbours of the anchor at `nearby` and tier-2 at `distant` (`spatialDigest.js` `tiers`), each demoted one band where `hopWeeks` exceeds the realm's median primary hop × 1.5 (DRAFT, spectrum-relative per the host's H2); aspatially graph hop 1 `nearby`, hop 2 `distant` — a PROXY: the relationship graph links courts that deal with each other (trade partners may lie a realm apart), so aspatially "attached" means near in the world's dealings, not on the ground; the canonized arm is the geographic one (PLAUSIBLE: the graph's density is not measured here) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-32 (2)]. Nothing beyond `distant` is attached.

### 2.2 The catalog — ten kinds, a free name (closed: `SITE_KINDS`)

| Kind | What it covers (display nouns are DISPLAY ONLY, never a rule) | The map's word (V14, per the host document) |
|---|---|---|
| `fortification` | keep, fort, castle, motte, redoubt | "fort" (the host document's "a ruin, fort, camp or crypt"; H13 "held sites (forts, watchposts)") |
| `watchtower` | watchtower, signal tower, watchpost | "watchpost" (H13) |
| `tower` | a mage's tower, an arcane outpost | — (a V14 kind name, if any, maps here) |
| `shrine` | shrine, abbey, holy place, hermitage (J384 (10): DM-placed, deity-tagged) | holy place |
| `waystation` | inn, tollhouse, caravanserai, coaching post (always on a road) | — |
| `crossing` | bridge, ford, pass (always on a road) | the canon's crossings are H5/H22's travel slot; the GATE is the digest's and its keeper the territory's holder (J385; PS2); a crossing site is a held structure at that gate, never its keeper [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-2 · HOST-r2-3] |
| `resource` | mine, quarry, logging camp (adopts a `deriveResourceSites` location by `resourceKey`) | "camp" is the host document's word with no defined meaning (host law 10): its mapping is the map program's (RULINGS-ROUND2 D Q1; the fold's map INBOX (i)) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-30 (2)] |
| `hospice` | hospice, leper house, infirmary | — |
| `harbour_works` | lighthouse, mole, quay — only on an ELIGIBLE PORT (`distanceRead.js :: isPort`, M8's geography ∧ institutions); the works never confer eligibility, and a navy is W-NAVY's (an institution IN the town), never a site [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-3 · HOST-r2-20] | — |
| `ruin` | dungeon, barrow, crypt, ruined city (quality fixed `ruined`; re-kinding is ONE `SITE_KIND` act that keeps the id — `rekinded`, §6.2 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-8 (3)]) | "ruin", "crypt" |

NO `lair` kind (the chair's ruling 14): a menace, a gang or a beast in ANY site makes it a lair or a den in prose (a conclave's site is a shrine or a hermitage in prose, never a lair — A.3); when it is broken or leaves, the site is unheld again. The map's own kind vocabulary was NOT reported to this seat (HOST-r1 lists none; V14 is the map's repository) — the mapping column above names only the words the host document quotes; the full alignment is OPEN QUESTION Q1 to the chair (one vocabulary where they meet; a map kind with no row here maps to the nearest kind or is refused as an invented fact, the host's law 10) — RULED (RULINGS-ROUND2 D Q1): ST maps the host document's words now (fort · watchpost · ruin · crypt · camp · holy place); V14's full list is asked of the map program. STEADINGS ARE NOT SITES (J384 (11): members of the parent's realm drawn at their frozen cells).

### 2.4 The derived reads (signatures; each pure, memoized per snapshot)

- `siteAt(worldState, siteId) → SiteView | null` — the record plus `state`, `fundingBand`, `attachments`, `effectClass` (`tied · untied · none`), `mightBand`. READERS: the dossier's site page, the hearing card's WHERE (`site`), PL's garrison arm (`riders_quartered` (iii), PL §4.7 F.6 (b)) and `closeSiteWeek` [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-13 (WC-ST-1)].
- `holderSinceOf(worldState, siteId) → tick | null` — the tick the CURRENT holder came to hold the site (`holderSince`, §2.1), `null` when unheld; EXPORTED for THE BODIES' tenure read (BD's hamlet end), never the `events` ring [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-93)].
- `sitesNear(worldState, settlementId, digest?) → [{ siteId, kind, band, effectClass }]` — every site attached to a settlement, codepoint-sorted (THE SITE READ of ST18).
- `settlementFundingBand(worldState, settlementId, snapshot) → FUNDING_BAND` — THE ONE settlement band: its `prosperityRank01` (`< 0.2` unfunded · `< 0.45` meagre · `< 0.8` funded · else lavish, DRAFT), with ONE input row under THE BODIES' `bodyEffectsEnabled` ONLY: a STANDING BOUNTY the court has posted (BD §9.9) lowers the court's band ONE STEP (never below `unfunded`) for each season it stands — THE BOUNTY'S COUNTERFORCE, the band taking the input and BD never writing it; because this is THE ONE band, every reader sees the lowered step — PR's pay gate, CH's retainers and the court's OWN SITES through `fundingBandOf` (its keeps' want clock and readiness): a court that spends on a bounty has less for its walls, by the one-band law (no second, unspent band for the keeps); the receipt on each reader's line names the bounty; with `bodyEffectsEnabled` dark the row is absent and the band byte-identical [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify RU-BD-r5 (b) · SEAMS-r5-23 · RULINGS-ROUND6 B.7 · `cure/D-BD-r5.md` §5 ST (b): the route arrived after ST's lane closed. REFINED, vetoable: the step REACHES site funding (the verifier's "decide also"), because exempting the keeps would be a second band] — exported for THE PATROLS' pay gate (PR §3.1) and CH's retainers [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-12: PR needs a settlement-keyed band; the site read was keyed by site] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 2 · PR-V6 (A.9): ONE name, this one; PR's `fundingBandOfSettlement` is renamed in PR (routed)].
- `fundingBandOf(worldState, siteId, snapshot) → FUNDING_BAND` — the EFFECTIVE band `min(fundingSet, derived)` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-1]; derived by holder: a settlement by `settlementFundingBand`; a SEAT's clergy by its creed's standing band in that seat town (`cult` unfunded · `established` funded · `ascendant` lavish — the faith contest's landed standing words, PLAUSIBLE until ST-3's car-0 names the field); a person by CH's derived purse band (a slot until CH lights; `meagre` meanwhile); a menace and an id-less authored gang `unfunded`; a BODY holder (gang or conclave) by its OWN supply stock — THE REPAIR CEILING (L-BD 31: "they lack the logistics"): `BODY_FUNDING_BY_SUPPLY` maps THE BODIES' supply word (`bodyReadiness01`'s `BODY_SUPPLY_WORDS`, BD §2.5) `provisioned` → `meagre` · `strained` → `unfunded` · `starving` → `unfunded`, so a body's band is NEVER above `meagre` — EXCEPT a conclave whose creed an attached court holds `endowed` (THE BODIES' `courtStanceOf`; RULINGS-ROUND3 §G.2: the one thing patronage buys), which repairs with that court's logistics: its band is the HIGHEST `settlementFundingBand` among the endowing courts attached to the site (codepoint tie-break) — THE PATRON'S LIFT; a BEAST is `unfunded` always and never repairs (L-BD 31: "Beasts never repair it"); with THE BODIES dark, every body holder reads `unfunded` (its sites only wear) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — L-BD 31 · BD's route: the body holder's band, its ceiling and the patron's lift; `D-ST-r4-probe/census-r4.out` (`BODY_FUNDING_BY_SUPPLY` FREE); the readiness each band gives, `D-ST-r4-probe/arith.out`]. The DM's `fundingSet` is a ceiling over every one of these (ST6).
- `siteTermAt(worldState, settlementId, seam, snapshot) → { value, sites: [siteId], reason }` — THE ONE LEAF every host calls (§3.3): the bounded lean of every site attached to that settlement on that seam, with the receipt naming the sites.
- `sitePresenceFeeder(worldState, settlementId) → { value, siteId }` and `siteSuppression(worldState, settlementId) → { value, siteId }` — the danger ledger's feeder and suppressor (§3.4). ONE TERM PER THING (BD's route (2)): where a site's untied holder IS a body and `bodyEffectsEnabled` is lit, the feeder reads ZERO for that site — THE BODIES' row `bodyPresenceFeeder` carries that body's presence (BD §8.2: "a body at an ST den does not count twice"); with `bodyEffectsEnabled` dark (THE BODIES unbuilt or dark-after-lit) the feeder reads the body holder as any untied holder, its might the body RECORD's (read, never copied) — the dark arm BD §15 names ("ST's holder refs read as untied holders with the record's `might`") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (2)].
- `denPressOf(worldState, siteId, settlementId) → number` — how hard the site's holder presses ONE attached town: ST's own feeder term for that site at that town, or, where the handoff above gives a body's presence to THE BODIES, that body's own term, THE BODIES' `bodyPresenceTermOf(worldState, bodyId, settlementId)` (BD §8.2: "`PRESENCE_BY_MIGHT[might] × ACT_FACTOR[act]` for that one body at that town, before the MAX and the suppression") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 5 · BD-r4-23: the per-body read cited by its name, which BD gave it in round 4] — so the clearing fork and the den's occasion (§6.1 step 6, §6.6) read the ONE term the composer carries, never a second computation of it [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the handoff would otherwise blind step 6 and §6.6, whose test "the ST presence feeder on that town ≥ 0.40" reads zero for every body's den once THE BODIES' effects light]. It is the read for an UNTIED holder only: a CONCLAVE holds as `faith`, a tied holder with no hazard term (§3.1), so no fork ever reads its press — a flock's site is SEIZED by the court's STANCE (§6.1 step 6c), never by press [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1 · L-BD 30 (V2 fail): step 6 gated the seizure on this read, which is zero for every conclave, so the seizure could never fire].
- `siteWitnesses(worldState, place) → [settlementId]` — the holders whose held watchtower watches a place (a stretch by the one stretch key `stretch:<a|b>`, the digest's `pairKey` form — A.14 — or a country) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 5: the harbour works' arm struck; A.14: the key named]: a REGISTERED INPUT of THE PATROLS' `witnessedCrossing` (PR §6.5 already reads it), never a witness set of its own and never a `partyIds` write (ST17) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-11].
- `siteReadinessOf(worldState, siteId, snapshot) → { siteReadiness01, word }` — THE ONE READINESS VALUE of a held site (A.10): `siteReadiness01 = F(fundingBandOf) × Q(quality)` (§3.2's factors; × ½ for a `person` or `faith` holder of a `fortification`, §3.2), and its word on `SITE_READINESS` (§2.3); an unheld site has none (ST7). For an UNTIED holder the value is the WALLS manned at that holder's means (a menace `unfunded`; a body by its supply word, never above `meagre` — the repair ceiling) and enters the site's own siege and clearing contests (§8); its card word stays the holder's might, never a garrison word (below) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (3) · L-BD 31]. Every reader of a garrison reads THIS: the town's keep term, the site's own siege, the map's word [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3 (A.10): one value, one scale, one word]. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the value is minted `siteReadiness01`, not the ruling's `readiness01`: `readiness01` is LANDED as a town's and an army's MARTIAL readiness (14 src files; `WarTab.jsx :: readinessWords` prints "on a war footing" from it), and ST9 reads that index beside this one — two meanings of one token in one volume would be the homonym A.10 forbids (`cure/D-ST-r3-probe/census-r3.out`).]
- `siteDefenseOf(worldState, settlementId, snapshot, deployments) → number` — the keep's `homeDefense` term: the MAX over the `fortification` sites attached at `at_hand` that pass the attacker-free holder rule (§4.1) of `KEEP_DEFENSE_MAX` (18, DRAFT) × `siteReadiness01` — the same value the site's own siege reads (§8) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3 (A.10)].
- `garrisonBandOf(worldState, siteId, snapshot) → SITE_READINESS word` — a held `fortification`'s or `watchtower`'s garrison word: `siteReadinessOf(…).word` (the map's own phrase, "held sites (forts, watchposts) with holder and garrison band", kept as the read's name so the join reads as the map asks; what it returns is a READINESS word, never an army band); the map's H13 `PolitySite` reads it, and the patrol round trip ("measured from the nearest garrisoned seat or site") counts a held site above `hollow` as garrisoned [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-9] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3 (A.10): round 2's `100 × F × Q` on `STRENGTH_BANDS`' floors in MN's keys is STRUCK — it called a funded sound keep `formidable` (80 ≥ 78) while its siege defended at 14.4 latent points, under the `thin` floor (`readiness.out`)]. An UNTIED holder has no garrison word: its card word is its might on MN's keys (it is a force, and the might word is a force's word).
- `believedDenOf(worldState, courtId, siteId, snapshot) → { known, holderRef?, mightBand?, sinceTick?, source? }` — what a COURT believes of a site held by a FORCE it may contest: an untied holder (a gang, a beast, a menace, an id-less authored gang) or a conclave whose creed the court holds `curbed` or `outlawed` (the seizure, §6.1 step 6c) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1: `curbed` added]. ST STORES NO BELIEF (A.5: "ST's record keeps only the TRUTH of the holder"): this is ONE READ BY PLACE OVER THE COURT'S OWN BELIEFS (A.17 · RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2) — the court's entries in MN's `believedMenaces(worldState, courtId)` whose newest place is this site (`lastSiteId`) together with its entries in THE BODIES' list form `believedBodies(worldState, courtId)` whose `baseId` is this site (L-BD 45's record: believed size band · base · last heard), both composed from the ONE sighting store MN owns (`spatialLedgers.sightings`, keyed court × subject `{ kind: 'menace' | 'body', id }`, its one writer `recordSighting` — B.2) and the tellings; the NEWEST by tick wins, and the ARM (menace or body) and the subject's id come from THAT belief, NEVER from the site's current truth holder; an id-less authored gang through the told arm alone (below) — never a live truth read and never a second belief; `known` is true while that belief places a force AT THIS SITE [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify A.5 FAIL (MAJOR: "the sighting store moved, but the READ is still keyed by the TRUTH holder" — step 1 clears the holder when a body leaves, so no id was left to read, and S36's re-pointed holder sent the read to MN's arm where the Hill Wolves were unheard) · RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2 · SEAMS-r5-3: "`believedDenOf` picks its arm from the court's sighting, so a body that left unseen is still believed present until a new sighting or telling"], so a holder that LEFT unseen is still believed there, stale, until a newer sighting or a telling reaches the court (Law One: belief outlives truth — A.5), and a fork that acts on it meets the truth at the site (below). THE SIGHTING (`source: 'seen'`) — A WRITE INTO THE COURT'S BELIEF, NEVER ONTO THE SITE (A.5): at every event that installs a body or menace holder at a site that is NOT `keptSecret` — an arrival's `taken`, an authored placement, a `site_siege` fall, a body's own take, the winning side's install after a won `site_cleared` (§6.1 step 6d), `made_known` of a held site — the one writer RETURNS the sightings the event occasions, one `{ courtId, holderRef, siteId, mightBand, tick }` per court attached `at_hand` (the anchor; both ends of a road), the might band SEEN at that tick, and its caller (ST's stage and its closing call, the realm verb arm, THE BODIES' stage through `arriveAt`) hands each to MN's ONE door `recordSighting(worldState, { courtId, subject: { kind, id }, mightBand, tick, whereId, siteId })` — THE ONE SIGHTING STORE `spatialLedgers.sightings` (RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2: MN's `menaceSightings` generalized to any subject; MN owns the record, its door, its closed callers and its horizon; `believedMenaces` and THE BODIES' `believedBodyOf`/`believedBodies` READ it) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — B.2: two owners' doors became one]; the same write follows every later witness of the holder AT THE SITE — a contest a court fights there (`site_taken`'s sortie, `site_cleared`, `site_siege`: each contesting court's row, the band it met) and a PATROL'S LOOK (THE PATROLS §9.2 (b): the looking court's row, written by PR through the same door `recordSighting`, never through ST's writer); a witness AWAY from the site (a raid on the court's land, the warden's office knowledge, L-BD 41) writes into the same one store by the same door (B.2: "PR's look and the at-hand witness write through it"). A sighting is the COURT's, never the site's: no holder change drops it. So the at-hand courts know the holder in the arrival's OWN week, in every preset — and the sighting is a written belief row, not a telling, for a MEASURED reason: the rumor stage seeds a tick's news BEFORE the calamity stage runs (`pulseKernel.js`: "`const rumors = advanceRumorLedgers({`" at 2097, "`const calamity = advanceCalamity({`" at 2527; re-read, `D-ST-r5-probe/master-reads-r5.out` m1), so a telling written at the act reaches even its hop-0 holders a tick late [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — R1, kept: a row, not a telling] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5 · the ruling's ST-X3 (V1): round 4 wrote the row onto ST's own `holder.seenBy`, whose life was the holder's TENURE — a refreshed band reverted to an older telling at an unperceived departure, and a kept-secret den's witnessing court lost its whole belief the tick the den went; now the row is the court's, in the holder's own record, and outlives the departure]. A NAMELESS BODY (below THE BODIES' naming band — BD23: "no town keeps a belief record of it") is still SEEN at a site on a court's own land: its sighting row is the one exception BD23 must admit, keyed by the body's id with no name (the one store keys by subject id, so the row needs no name — B.2), or every nameless den would be unanswerable by every court (ROUTE BD) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5's seam with BD23, stated so it is carried]. AN ID-LESS AUTHORED GANG (before THE BODIES land: DM-placed only, so it never arrives, never besieges and is never seen arriving) has no belief owner, so courts believe it by the TOLD arm ALONE — its authored `site_taken` row (cause `dm`) carries the DM's might through MN17's might↔magnitude map, the anchor its hop-0 holder from the next tick; a look or a contest at its den writes nothing ST could keep (ST stores no belief; a court-scoped telling is no landed rumor idiom, `master-reads-r5.out` m4), and at a `keptSecret` site no court knows it until `made_known` — the interval's honest gap; BD-0's adoption hands nothing over: THE BODIES' record of the adopted gang begins from the tellings each court holds (ROUTE BD) [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the id-less arm under A.5]. THE TOLD ARM (`source: 'told'`) — ITS ONE CARRIER OF THE SITE: a telling names the site through ONE `siteId` content slot on the rumor network's `spatial/rumorNetwork.js :: captureContent` (module-private; today `whereId: ids.length ? ids[0] : ''` and no `siteId` in the module — measured), MN17's typed-slot idiom, minted by the fold's IN INBOX row with its seeding terms (the significance a held site's arrival carries; an authored `cause dm` placement told to the courts, never `audience: 'dm-only'`), ROUTED; until that row lands the told arm reaches a court as its anchor's news and `known` reads false for a site the telling cannot name [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-47; RULINGS-P5 C.17: "IN's `captureContent` gains ONE `siteId` content slot (MN17's idiom — a fold IN row + ST cites)")]: every other court — in the omniscient arm the truth from the tick after the act; in the belief presets the newest TELLING of the site it holds, whichever made or unmade the holder — `site_taken` (whose one writer is ST's, for the hearing's verdict AND an authored placement, cause `dm`), a `site_siege` fall, MN17's menace rumors for a menace holder, THE BODIES' rumor slots (`bodyId` · `bodyKind`, BD §2.4) for a body holder (the arrival's `site_taken` row carries MN17's typed `menaceId`/`menaceName` or BD's `bodyId`/`bodyKind`, so the told arm feeds the same one belief read), and against them the UNMAKING telling — ONE `site_cleared` news row, its word `cleared · driven_off · abandoned · ended` in the content, emitted by the one writer at EVERY event that ends a force's hold (a clearing, a menace's end, a body's end or departure, PT's `party_cleared_site`, the DM's `SITE_HOLDER` to none) — the anchor as the involved settlement, so the at-hand courts hold the unmaking telling as its hop-0 holders from the next tick (read-last, write-next); the might through MN17's might↔magnitude map or BD's slots; unheard ⇒ not known; a won `site_cleared` whose winning side is a FORCE (a body, a menace — §6.1 step 6d) emits its ONE unmaking row with the CLEARER's typed slots aboard, so one row tells the old holder gone and the new one holding (one fact, one row) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3: the clearer now holds]. A FORK ON A STALE BELIEF MEETS THE TRUTH AT THE SITE: a sortie, a seizure or a reclaim's clearing decided on a belief the holder has since outrun is resolved by its contest on the TRUTH it finds (the resolver resolves the physics — CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)): an EMPTY site is `cleared` with no draw and the clearer holds it (§6.1 step 6d); a DIFFERENT holder is contested as the holder it is where the court holds it hostile, else the force turns home (`repelled`, no draw, no loss; the receipt says what it found); either way the contesting court's sighting is written [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5's consequence for ST's own forks, so a stale belief never fights a ghost and never goes uncorrected]. THE VEIL (A.16): a `keptSecret` site emits NO telling — no arrival row, no authored-placement row, no `site_siege` fall, no unmaking row — and occasions no sighting, until `made_known` (the DM's `SITE_STATUS`; the one writer then emits the telling of its current holder, cause `dm`, and returns the at-hand courts' sightings); until then a court learns of a kept-secret den ONLY by WITNESS — a patrol's look (PR), the warden (L-BD 41), the holder's own act seen on that court's land — written into the holder's one belief for THAT court alone, never a telling; a deed of the party at a kept-secret site names the anchor and the act, never the site, until it is made known (PT P56 — PT's half of the veil, cited, `cure/D-PT-r4.md` §5); in the omniscient arm a kept-secret site is known to no court BY THE TRUTH ARM, while a WITNESS still informs the court that saw, for that court alone — the witness wins [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 6 · D-PT-r4's route] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.17 · ST-W2 (MAJOR): round 3's "a court in `seenBy` knows the holder, its might as it stands" read the menace's might live every tick (ST §2.1: "`might` is READ from it, never copied") while MN's relief read `believedMenaces` — two beliefs of one might; now the band seen at the tick, and ONE read per holder kind · A.16 · ST-W3 (MAJOR): the told arm had no `keptSecret` guard — the arrival and the authored row fired for a kept-secret shrine, so far courts were told while the at-hand court was blind · note 2: every unmaking event now emits its telling] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-15] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V1 (RULINGS-ROUND3 B: no court could believe an arrival in its own week, so the folded contest could never fire — now the at-hand courts see it; the folded contest stands for them alone, §8) · ST-V2 (RULINGS-ROUND3 B: an authored den and a siege-taken keep made no telling, so every belief preset left them unanswerable — now every telling that makes an untied holder is read, and an authored placement EMITS the same `site_taken` row, one writer one kind)].

## §3 THE EFFECT TABLE AND ITS LAWS

### 3.1 The sign is the holder's

| Holder | Effect class | Who feels it |
|---|---|---|
| `settlement` (garrison or civil) · `faith` (a seat's clergy of a creed) · `person` (a ledger person; a paragon whose living `polarity` is `champion`) | `tied` — BENEFITS | the attached towns, by distance band |
| `faith` held by a CONCLAVE (THE BODIES' flock, ref `{ kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'conclave', id }`) | `tied` — BENEFITS, PER TOWN BY THAT TOWN'S STANCE TOWARD THE CREED | each attached town by distance band WHERE its court's stance toward the creed (THE BODIES' one read `courtStanceOf(worldState, snapshot, settlementId, 'creed:<ref>')`, whose default is the owner's alignment word — the patron's and the flock's gods' `stanceOf` distance, BD §7.1) is `permitted` or `endowed`; a town that holds the creed `curbed` or `outlawed` takes NOTHING from it (neither benefit nor hazard — it shuts the flock out); whatever harm a HOSTILE flock does (a creed a town curbs or outlaws may RAID that town for supplies and bodies and take captives, its god's axes a weight — RULINGS-ROUND5 B.2) is its own ACTS through THE BODIES' rows, one term per thing (BD §9.2's raid, BD §9.10's captives) — and THE BODIES' presence row counts a conclave at a town ONLY in a raid on that town, never at its shrine and never at a town that permits or endows its creed (ROUTED to BD as V2's ST-X2 cure: BD §8.2's row as written takes "the MAX over the bodies whose `where` touches T … a body at a site attached `at_hand` to T touches T" with "`ACT_FACTOR` … based in the country 0.8", so a `permitted` flock at its shrine would feed the danger of the very town that welcomes it once `bodyEffectsEnabled` lights) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2's ST-X2: ST's citation of BD §8.2 for "a HOSTILE flock" misread BD's quantifier; the exclusion ST relies on is now stated and routed]; the faith arm (the holding creed's local strength, §3.3 shrine) reaches the permitting and endowing towns only — in a curbing or outlawing town THE BODIES' stance multiplier on that creed's strength (BD §7.4) is the one lean (one term per thing); with THE BODIES dark the stance read is absent and the flock's site reads as any faith holder of its creed |
| `untied` (a gang or a beast — THE BODIES; a menace; an id-less authored gang before C17) · a `person` whose paragon `polarity` is `villain` | `untied` — the GENERAL HAZARD (ST8) | EVERY attached town, by distance band; no town benefits |
| none | `none` — NOTHING (ST7) | nobody |

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · SEAMS-r3-1 (BLOCKING; its cure: "a creed-held site's sign and might read the holder creed's standing in the attached town and the alignment distance between its deity and the town's … never a blanket hazard") · ST-W1: "a cult" STRUCK from the `untied` row; a conclave's site is `faith`, tied, its sign per town read from THE BODIES' one stance read — which IS the alignment distance by default (BD §7.1's conclave default: `stanceOf(the town's PATRON god, the flock's god)`, `net = cooperation − aggression`) — so a good flock's shrine helps a good town and a far god's flock is shut out by a town that curbs it, never a hazard to anyone.]

### 3.2 The scale (every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster)

- TIED term — an ADDITIVE term is `BASE[kind][seam] × S`, and a GATE ROW (a multiplier) scales its DEVIATION from one, `mult = 1 + (BASE − 1) × S`, where `S = F(funding) × Q(quality) × D(distance)` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-7 (RULINGS-ROUND2b): the literal product on a multiplier flipped its sign — an unfunded keep's ×0.8 read ×0.12 and a capable den's ×1.3 read ×0.78; now ×0.97 and ×1.18 (`cure/D-ST-r2-probe/scaleAndLatch.out`), never a sign flip]; `F`: unfunded 0.15 ("walls without defenders") · meagre 0.45 · funded 0.8 · lavish 1.0; `Q`: sound 1 · worn 0.5 · ruined 0; `D`: at_hand 1 · nearby 0.5 · distant 0.2. A `person` or `faith` holder of a `fortification` counts at half (a lord-errant's hall is not a garrison).
- UNTIED term — additive `BASE_H × S_H`, gate row `1 + (BASE_H − 1) × S_H`, with `S_H = M(might) × Qu(quality) × D(distance)`; `M`: thin 0.2 · modest 0.4 · capable 0.6 · strong 0.8 · formidable 1.0 (MN's might words, the J-CNC-5 keys); `Qu`: sound 1 · worn 0.65 · ruined 0.35 (a ruined site gives the mildest hazard, the owner's second word as the chair ruled). So "a sound keep of brigands at the gate is a crisis and a crumbling barrow three days off is a rumour" (ruling 16b).
- Per seam, per town: the terms of several sites COMBINE BY MAX within a sign (no stacking; requirement 2) and the result is clamped to the seam's cap below.
