### 3.4 The danger ledger: ST's feeder and suppressor in MN's ONE presence term (ruling 21; MN5)

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-8 · SEAMS-r2-7 · ST-r2-28 · RULINGS-ROUND2b (MN's routed note): the composition's OWNER is MN — its leaf `presenceTerms` and its one composition sentence (MN §3.3, §4.2); this section states ST's two registered inputs and quotes that sentence, never a second one.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 3 · PR-r2-8 · PR-V2 (A.1) · SEAMS-r2-7 (the MN verifier's (b) and (c), ST's mirror) · MN-V6 (A.3): round 2 restated MN's composition with ST's own names (`wallsTerm` meaning every unsuppressible part, where MN keeps `wallsTerm` and `columnTerm` apart), still called the column "the chair's call" after the ROUND2b ADDENDUM settled it, zeroed the fort's suppression while THE PATROLS are lit, and said a kept road may let a raided country "fall out of the latch" — all four withdrawn; this section now CITES.] THE ONE COMPOSITION IS MN's: `presenceTerms`, MN §3.3 — "`presenceTerms` IS THE ONE COMPOSITION SENTENCE (every volume cites it, none restates it)" — composed inside `embattlement.js :: advanceEmbattlement` before the step (it receives worldState; zero kernel lines), built as A.3's SHARED CAR: the first of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 to land builds it, the rest register, and it lights when ANY registered feeder's own flag is lit (ST's two inputs under `siteEffectsEnabled`, §9), each input gated by its own flag. ST REGISTERS TWO INPUTS AND NOTHING ELSE: (1) `sitePresenceFeeder(T)` — the MAX over the untied sites attached to T of §3.3's feeder BASE_H × S_H — into MN's SUPPRESSIBLE part `roadCountry(T)` (a den is never at the walls and is never a column); (2) `siteSuppression(T)` — the MAX over the held suppressor sites attached to T (`fortification` · `watchtower` · `waystation` · `crossing` · `harbour_works`, each §3.3's BASE × S) — into MN's one suppressor slot σ (MN §3.3's sentence: the registered suppressors summed under the one 0.25 cap — cited, never restated [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — note 3's residue]), beside THE PATROLS' slow subtraction (PR §6.1). ST's feeder hands a body's den to THE BODIES' row while `bodyEffectsEnabled` is lit (§2.4, §3.3 — one term per thing); THE BODIES' row registers into the same suppressible part (`roadCountry`, MN §3.3's closed list), so a keep and riders suppress a gang's presence exactly as they suppress a den's (BD §8.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (2)]. The keep's suppression stands PATROLS lit or dark: its garrison is the site's own complement and the riders are the town's, two feeders of one capped σ — different men, so never a double count (A.1; ST10). What MN's sentence never suppresses ST never touches: the walls term and an enemy column (the ADDENDUM: OD-PS-19 STANDS — "forts and riders never suppress a hostile ARMY's presence"). What the kept road does and does not do is ST8's (EXECUTED, `cure/D-ST-r3-probe/latchExit.out`): it keeps a calm town calm where the suppressed raid part stays at or below the latch, and it never ends an embattlement the raid still feeds above `EXIT_THRESHOLD` 0.30. Where anything here and MN's sentence ever differ, MN's governs and this one is corrected. Every danger consumer then follows by construction (§7.1). Dormancy: off the spatial marker the ledger is a no-op (the landed gate) — so the danger arm is CANONIZED-ONLY (TIER lines say so); the gates, the counterforce slot, the stressor-layer care, the faith and the defence arms are aspatial-safe; the pestilence front's care is canonized-only (§3.3).

### 3.5 The crisis counterpart, per kind (ruling 18; ST11)

| Kind | The function | Its crisis | The one read | What the crisis spends first |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fortification` | defence | a siege; a menace at the walls | `homeDefense` (`siteDefense`, LARGE) | the keep's quality (`breached`: sound → worn → ruined), §4.2 |
| `hospice` | health | an outbreak in the town (the stressor) or the pestilence front at it | the outbreak's counterforce slot and the front's care product (§3.3) — each acts only while the plague stands [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-12: the ×2.5 multiplier struck; the cap holds] | the hospice's capacity: one `worn` step when an outbreak in an attached `at_hand` town peaks while held (DRAFT) |
| `shrine` | faith | an unseating; a `religious_conversion_fracture` in the town | the seated creed's strength (the shrine's lean ×2.5 while the fracture stands, if the shrine's creed is the seated one) | the shrine's standing: a `worn` step when the fracture resolves against its creed; `destroyed` when an untied force (a gang, a beast, a menace) takes it [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3: "an untied heretic" STRUCK] |
| `tower` | magic | `magic_deadzone` · `magical_instability` in the town | the arcane counterforce (+0.15 — it acts only in the crisis) | the wards: a `worn` step when the crisis resolves in that town while held |
| `watchtower` | watch | an incursion, a spy ring, a quiet crossing | THE PATROLS' `witnessedCrossing`, through its registered input (ST17) | the tower's watch: a `worn` step when an untied force takes a site at hand unseen |
| `crossing` | supply and the march | a siege's supply lines; a relief column's route | `scoreRoute`; the army supply read (`warCapacityReads.js :: logisticsBurdenFor`, PLAUSIBLE, named at ST-3's car-0) | the bridge: `destroyed` when an enemy army cuts it (ST-2b) or the DM says so |
| `harbour_works` | sea trade and the sea gate | a blockade (`navalLayer.js :: activeBlockadeTargets` holds its port); piracy | the danger at the port node — NO drastic term (the blockade is W-NAVY's siege) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-3] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 5: "and the sea gate's witness" struck] | the works: a `worn` step when a blockade has held its port a season (DRAFT 13 weeks, UNMEASURED) |
| `resource` | yield | a famine or a dearth of its good | ⛔ STOP-ST-2 | the working (claim-jumped: an untied holder takes it) |
| `waystation` | trade on the road | a route disruption | `scoreRoute` ONLY, modestly — NO drastic term | the station (a roost: an untied holder takes it) |
| `ruin` | none | none | none | — |

[CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-10 (WC-ST-41; RULINGS-P5 C.10): the last column had ONE writer — the keep's breach (§4.2) — and §6.1's steps read none of the other six triggers; every spend above now has its writer, §6.1 step 8 THE CRISIS SPENDS, one step per crisis, each read and flag named there, the keep's spend the template]

### 3.6 The three laws of the effects (ruling 16)

(a) Every positive names its counterforce (ST12; the per-kind rows of §5). (b) The untied hazard scales by the holder's MIGHT × quality × distance (§3.2). (c) Every effect has exactly ONE felt sentence (the gate row's reason, or §14's line) and ONE seam; no site has a truth of its own.

---
## §4 THE FORTIFICATION — THE FIRST FELT CHAIN (ST-1; ruling 22)

### 4.1 The ready keep in the one defence read (the owner's fifth word; ruling 17a)

**Reads:** TRUTH — the site record, the holder's effective funding band and the live one-army ledger (a physical fact of the world, like the walls). **Writes:** nothing (a derived term). **Seam (the envelope):** `warCapacityReads.js :: buildCapacityLookup(snapshot, deployments)` (143 eff): the envelope gains `siteDefense = siteDefenseOf(snapshot.worldState, id, snapshot, deployments)` and `homeDefense = Math.max(0, offensive − armyAway) + siteDefense` (+2 host lines); `computeAllyRelief` reads `homeDefense − siteDefense` for each ally (+1 line). `siteDefenseOf` = for the `fortification` sites attached to the town at `at_hand`, the MAX of `KEEP_DEFENSE_MAX × siteReadiness01` = `18 × F × Q` latent points, × ½ for a `faith` holder (§3.2; a `person` holder and a conclave never pass the holder rule below — neither is a settlement) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — note 5: the ½ §2.4 applies was missing here] (DRAFT, UNMEASURED: one `STRENGTH_BANDS` step on the 0..100 latent scale whose floors are 78/60/42/26/0 — a ready keep lifts the DEFENDER about one band; the army-away penalty it outweighs is `ARMY_DEPLOYED_CAPACITY_PENALTY = 14`); the keep's own garrison word is `siteReadiness01`'s on `SITE_READINESS`, never that band (A.10) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3]. **The holder rule, attacker-free** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-6: the lookup is cached per id with no attacker argument, so the exclusion is stated in what the lookup CAN see]: a keep counts when its holder is the town itself, or a settlement whose landed relationship with the town is a SUPPORT type (a seat's `faith` holder is judged by its seat; a body or a person never passes) (`ALLY_SUPPORT_TYPES` — `allied · vassal · patron · defensive_pact`, the set `computeAllyRelief` reads; module-local in `warCapacityReads.js`, so the host passes its own set to the leaf — never a second copy), whose sworn link is not suspended (PL's `swornLinkSuspended`, under `polityContestEnabled`), and whose own deployment does not target the town (`deployments[holder]?.targetId`, from the ledger the lookup already receives); any other holder's keep stands with nobody. So a crown's garrison never defends a member against the crown (a polity war suspends the link; a war of submission puts the crown's own deployment on the member), and a besieger's keep never defends its target. Car-0 pin: no settlement besieging T passes the rule for T (PLAUSIBLE until measured; should it fail, the fallback is a verdict-side subtraction beside `defenderReliefBonus`, +1 line). **The keep is the site's own garrison and walls, never the town's men** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-9] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 · PR-V2 · SEAMS-r2-9 (A.1): round 2's "the keep's standing watch is inside the town's own manpower (no second number)" is STRUCK]: ONE composition for the envelope — `offensive` (THE PATROLS' `patrolDraw` taken from it at deploy time, RULINGS-ROUND2b PR-r2-1) · `homeDefense = max(0, undrawn offensive − armyAway) + siteDefense` · relief `homeDefense − siteDefense`; the keep's garrison is the SITE's own complement, paid through the holder by the site's funding band and drawn from no town's manpower pool (so `offensive` never carries it and `patrolDraw` never takes it); it neither marches nor is drawn — the levy draws riders, never a keep; and the riders who walk its stretches are PR's, from the town's one pool (PR3). So: sound and lavishly kept → the full term (`at_strength`); worn or skeleton-manned ("walls without defenders") → small; ruined → none (`hollow`).

**The siege verdict reads it on BOTH cores** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-4 (RULINGS-ROUND2b): measured and executed, `cure/D-ST-r2-probe/siegeMonotone.out`]. ASPATIAL (the capacity roll): through `defenderCurrent` in `SIEGE_CAPACITY_K * (coalitionCurrent - defenderCurrent)` — pFall falls as the keep grows (executed: 0.8808 · 0.7389 · 0.5200 · 0.2932 for a keep of 0 · 6 · 12 · 18). SPATIAL (M5 siege-as-starvation, every canonized world): (a) the FEASIBILITY gate, the `SIEGE_MAX_AGE` ceiling and capitulation read `defenderCurrent`, which carries the keep through `homeDefense`; (b) the STARVATION CORE's holding power — walls and stores are a LONGER CLOCK: `timeFactor = clamp01(siegeAge / (STARVE_FULL_TICKS + KEEP_CLOCK_WEEKS_MAX × siteDefense / 18))`, `KEEP_CLOCK_WEEKS_MAX` 6 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED: a ready keep stretches a season of total interdiction by half a season); (c) the keep NEVER enters `relief01`: `relief01 = clamp01(relief / Math.max(1, defenderCurrent − (defenderCap.siteDefense || 0)))`, so a keep cannot shrink what allies bring (the landed line raised pFall from 0.0759 to 0.0937 with a +18 keep — executed). Host edits: `warSiegeVerdict.js` (180 eff; +2 lines — the clock and the denominator — reading `defenderCap.siteDefense` from the envelope it already receives). THE PIN (S19): over the probe's grid (2,000 cells of home defence, relief, siege age, interdiction and keep), a bigger keep never raises pFall. **Also by construction:** the defender-attrition ledger's seed, MN-2's menace siege (might vs `homeDefense`, MN-r1-1), and the deploy-time FEASIBILITY gates of `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` — its coalition arm and its ordinary-target arm both call `classifyFeasibility({ … defenderCurrent: defenderCap.homeDefense … })` — read the keep. **The chooser is BLIND to it** (stated, not cured): the `CONQUEST_MARGIN` prefilter and the softmax chooser read `buildStrengthLookup` (the relationship-confidence strength), not the envelope, so a ready keep deters a march only at the feasibility gate [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-24: `buildOriginEnvelope` is the reinforcement envelope and was the wrong cite]. **One coverage defect named:** `display/warResolve.js` recomputes "`const homeDefense = Math.max(0, offensive - (besieging.length ? ARMY_DEPLOYED_CAPACITY_PENALTY : 0));`" — ST-1 adds the same `siteDefenseOf` term there (one leaf, every reader; the §7 walker pins it). The dossier's defence page shows the keep's line beside the stored generated score (never inside `defenseLedger`, whose importers include the frozen `armyTransitKernel.js`).

### 4.2 Unscathed is a stock the siege spends (ruling 17b; ST11)

**Seam:** `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685 eff): the verdict is wrapped where it is made, BEFORE the attrition loop reads it — `const verdict = keepTakesTheBreach(worldState, targetId, resolveSiegeVerdict({ … }), snapshot, existing)` (+2 lines). **Only a ROLLED fall** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-5: requirement 24(b)'s hearing seal is "read by the resolver FIRST (before any feasibility, max-age or capitulation arm)" and "the verdict forces the hand that week"]: the leaf acts only when `verdict.falls && verdict.roll > 0` — the landed no-roll sentinel (the pin's own comment: "`roll` is 0, the same word the feasibility, ceiling and capitulation arms already write when no roll happened"; executed — the HBF-86 pin, the `SIEGE_MAX_AGE` storm and a capitulation each answer `roll: 0`, a rolled fall `roll: 0.001`, `cure/D-ST-r2-probe/siegeMonotone.out` (3)); so a fall the DM pinned or sealed, a capitulation and the ceiling's storm are never absorbed (a sealed verdict carries no roll by the same idiom when SP-9's seal lands; S20 pins it). When it acts and a qualifying keep at hand (§4.1's holder rule) is `sound` or `worn`, the fall lands on the KEEP: the verdict returns `falls: false` with its band rewritten to `narrow_fail` (a hold that came close to breaking), so the besiegers' attrition and the defender's ledger read a hold that agrees, with `keepBreached: siteId` in its receipt; a `breached` event (one quality step: `sound → worn`, `worn → ruined`) is queued; only when no keep qualifies does the town's own fall run. **The write** goes through ST's one writer inside the war layer's returned `worldStatePatch` (composed with razing's patch), which the kernel already merges ("`worldState = { ...worldState, ...war.worldStatePatch, … }`") — zero kernel lines. **ST-1's car-0 STOP condition:** if any `spatialLedgers` sub-ledger is written between the war call and that merge (kernel ~994 → ~1081) the patch would clobber it — then the FALLBACK: the war layer stamps `keepBreaches` on the besieger's own deployment record (the war's writer) and ST's stage applies the step the same tick, idempotent by (deployment, count) — a CONDITIONAL DECLARED SUB-FIELD of a LANDED record, `deployments[*].keepBreaches?` (a count, written only on that STOP), with its lifecycle row (§11 B′) and ⭐ the owner's visible row (OD-ST-53); absent unless the STOP fires [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-12 (WC-ST-86; RULINGS-P5 C.12: "`keepBreaches` and `noLook` → their declaring volumes"): the fallback field had no §11 row, no OD row and no registration — ST is its declaring volume]. **The card:** "Kell's keep stands ready." · "Kell's keep is breached; the town's walls still hold." A relieved or lifted siege leaves the keep as it stands; funding restores it over years (§6.1 step 2).

### 4.3 A fort on the approach delays the siege (ruling 17c)

A held `fortification` on the hostile unit's route — on the stretch it travels (`where.kind = 'road'`) or `at_hand` to the next node it enters — is its own `site_siege` (STANDING; §8) that the attacker must resolve or BYPASS before the town's siege begins. **Seam:** THE PASSAGE's TRANSIT LEAF STAGE (PS §4.1), where the fort is a REGISTRANT in the ONE ORDER PS §4.1 writes (border → fort → screen → `armies_meet`; ST keeps no order of its own); the fort is read there through `sitesNear`, and the unit is held by the stage's ONE HOLD HELPER `holdBeforeNode` (PS9) — ST keeps no arithmetic of its own — in the helper's FREEZE mode (the unit's position held exactly where the prior tick left it, one week, no refund) for a fort on the stretch it travels, and in its border-clamp mode for a fort at the node it passed this tick (A.2). Every such hold is bounded by PS9's Bound 1 (THE TERMINATION LAW, every registrant: `arrivalTick − sinceTick ≤ SIEGE_MAX_AGE − 1`); WHERE THE PASSAGE IS LIT it also SPENDS PS9's cumulative Bound 2 (`PASSAGE_HOLD_CAP_WEEKS = SIEGE_MAX_AGE − MAX_MARCH_WEEKS − 1`, 7 weeks at master; RULINGS-ROUND2b: the cap covers every hold of one march), and the approach `site_siege`'s ceiling is the REMAINING cap; with THE PASSAGE dark there is no march row, the fort's hold is bounded by Bound 1 alone, and its ceiling is the weeks Bound 1 leaves. When the ceiling is spent the unit bypasses (a route that moves it) or turns back — waiting is no longer offered, so the war layer's own siege ceiling keeps its margin (`armyTransit.js :: siegeArrivalGate`: "MAX_MARCH_WEEKS is strictly BELOW the war layer's SIEGE_MAX_AGE") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-10] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PS-r2-15 (A.2): round 2 had the fort "hold as it holds at a refused border" (the border clamp, which pushes a mid-hop unit FORWARD — the verifier's worked example) and spend Bound 2 under ST's own flag even where no march row exists; now the freeze mode, PS §4.1's one order, and Bound 2 only where THE PASSAGE is lit]. Bypass is a ROUTE CHOICE under PS's rules (the mover's believed cost, PS item 5: the fort's siege weeks enter the path's believed cost; a detour may cost weeks or be impossible). So a chain of forts buys a kingdom time and a rotten one buys nothing. **Honest dependency:** this arm waits on PS's transit leaf (and its STOP if that leaf has no host) and on ST-2b's deployer arm; the at-hand keep of §4.1 does not.

### 4.4 The fort's other leans and its counterforces

- **Internal security** (the owner's first word): the crime-side BIRTH gate rows of §3.3 (`criminalCorridorGate`, the infiltration gate), at `at_hand` and `nearby` — the fort adds no corridor counterforce term (the landed corridor counterforce carries no security institution; the watch's run-side term is THE PATROLS' deviation row in ST's slot) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-21]; WC-12's located-brigand contest IS THE BODIES' raid threshold (WC-12's located unit is BD's gang record, BD §19), which reads WC §1.8.1's ONE brigand-vs-town read-point `defenseLedger(settlement)` ("defense + internal security"; walls · watch) with THE AT-HAND KEEP'S READINESS on the site scale beside it — ST's `siteReadinessOf` for the qualifying keep (§4.1's holder rule), composed at THE BODIES' leaf, never inside `defenseLedger` (walker (4)) — and never `homeDefense`, the army capacity (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.3) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.3 (ST's citation): round 4 cited the threshold as "`homeDefense` WITH the keep" and "`siteTermAt(…, 'security')` beside it" — two ST terms in one read; now ONE, the keep's readiness, while the security gate rows stay on the stressor birth gates].
- **The raider counterforce** — a fort IS a defence institution on the land: +0.15 additive after `counterforceAssessment`'s blend for `monster_raider_pressure` (zero where no site — a new weighted source would have diluted every siteless town).
- **Danger:** the suppression of §3.4.
- **Counterforces (ST12):** UPKEEP — the funding band falls with the holder's prosperity and the keep wears (§6.1); RESENTMENT — a crown's garrison in a member's land is PL's control instrument (OD-PL-33): ST supplies the occasion and PL records it as ONE `riders_quartered` ROW — PL's shape-3 kind (the fealty record, the OBLIGATION family), priced ONCE as its GARRISON ARM (iii) with its one resentment patch (PL §4.7 F.6 (b) (iii)), never a weave incident and never a §15.9 incident word (RULINGS-ROUND5 A.8 re-homed it out of the weave; RULINGS-P5 D.4) [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — RULINGS-P5 D.2 · D.4 (ST l.309, ST12, §4.4): the stale "PL's sworn-edge writer records it as a typed decaying incident (the memory-weave idiom …; PL registers the incident word in its own commit)" struck — it priced the garrison a second time beside PL's row, against PL F.6 (b) "PL's own row, the OBLIGATION family, never a weave incident"; C.17's "into §15.9's closed list" is WITHDRAWN (D.4)]: a site held by a lord above the member on its chain, attached `at_hand` to the member and garrisoned above `hollow`, standing at the season's end, writes ONE `riders_quartered` row on the member's own sworn edge that season, episode `riders_quartered:<holderId>:<seasonStartTick>`, carrying the one resentment patch (PL's P58) — ST names the occasion and writes nothing [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-42)]; THE SIEGE IT INVITES — a held fort is a target (ST-2b); A TRAITOR AT THE GATE — ES's product, a provocateur's lean on `site_siege`'s draw (ES's inbox; slot — the fold's §3.1 ES INBOX row, ROUTED; DARK until ES carries it [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-43)]); A DEN IF WITHDRAWN — an abandoned keep is an unheld site a gang or a beast may take (§6.7).

| `watchtower` | upkeep; a blinded watch (`worn` when an untied force takes a site at hand unseen); the omniscient arm makes it moot (no rumor road) | "Thornwatch's watchers saw the column cross the ford." | C1 / C16 (THE PATROLS' `witnessedCrossing` reads it; IN's witness plane a slot) |

## §6 HOW A SITE CHANGES HANDS

### 6.1 The one stage (ST-0's car; `src/domain/sites/siteStage.js :: advanceSites`)

A LEAF called at the head of `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` right after MN's menace stage (+3 host lines: the call and the merge of `{ worldState, newsEntries, receipts }`), before the calamity's own flag gate; zero kernel lines. It runs every tick, in this fixed order, each step a call to ST's one writer with `causedBy` on every event:
1. **Holders that ended.** A settlement holder or anchor that no longer stands (`lifecycleStatusOf` non-empty, or absent from the campaign) → holder cleared if it was that settlement, `abandoned`, `lastHolder` lapses (ST14). A person holder the DM has killed (the ledger's verdict; death is never the engine's) → `abandoned`. A menace holder that ended, stilled, waned or moved on (MN's record) → the site unheld, `cleared`, `causedBy` the menace's own event (the MN-r1-2 release idiom). A BODY holder that ENDED (THE BODIES' `ended` row: `starved · scattered · dissolved · merged · disbanded`) → the site unheld, `cleared`; one that LEFT by its own choice (a `move` of its base, a release) → `abandoned`; each `causedBy` the body's own event (BD §15: "its site falls EMPTY through ST's writer"). Every one of these unmakings emits its unmaking telling (§2.4) unless the site is `keptSecret` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route · note 2]. A body ended `raised` — the DM's PROMOTION — is NOT an unmaking: its site is never emptied, told or offered; the holder is RE-POINTED to the new menace in the promotion's own act (§6.7, A.2) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2 · RU-MN-r4 (V2's ROUTED-UNAPPLIED fail; the ruling's RU-MN-1): `raised` stood in this list, so the DM's new menace lost its lair, the anchor was told the den was gone and `lastHolder` was offered a reclaim in the very tick it was raised].
2. **Wear and restoration** (on a YEAR crossing, keyed by year — the rite idiom; `seasonForTick(...).year`): a tied holder at `funded`/`lavish` → `restored` one band if below `sound`; `meagre` → holds; `unfunded` → `worn` one band; an unheld or untied site → `worn` one band every second year (DRAFT, UNMEASURED). A `ruin` never changes quality. THE REPAIR CEILING (L-BD 31: "there is a ceiling to how much conclaves and gangs can bolster or repair … for they lack the logistics. Beasts never repair it"): a BODY holder's band is its supply word's (never above `meagre`, §2.4), so it HOLDS what it took — a `provisioned` gang or conclave PATCHES a `ruined` site up to `worn` in that year and never higher (`BODY_REPAIR_CEILING` = `worn`, DRAFT, UNMEASURED — a body cannot make a keep sound), a `strained` or `starving` body's site wears as an unheld one (every second year), a BEAST never repairs (its lair only wears, every second year); a conclave under THE PATRON'S LIFT (an attached court holds its creed `endowed`, §2.4) repairs as a tied holder at the patron's band, up to `sound`; and the keep's readiness follows the band (a body's sound keep reads at most `manned`, `siteReadiness01` ≤ 0.45; a conclave's at most `sparse`, × ½ as a faith holder of a fortification — SAVE UNDER THE PATRON'S LIFT, where it reads the patron's band × ½: a `lavish` patron's sound keep 1.0 × 1 × ½ = 0.50, `manned`; a `funded` one's 0.40, `manned` — `D-ST-r5-probe/arith-r5.out`) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 4: "at most `sparse`" omitted the lift] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — L-BD 31 · RULINGS-ROUND4 B (ST): the brief's "propose `sound`, never `lavish`/`at_strength`'s top" measured against ST's bands — `sound` is the TOP of the three quality bands, so the ceiling sits on the FUNDING the body can give (≤ `meagre`) and on the REPAIR it can make (to `worn`), and `at_strength` (≥ 0.75) is out of a body's reach without a patron].
3. **Abandonment by want** (the owner's "abandonded due to lower prosperity"; ruling 11a): a tied holder whose EFFECTIVE band (ST6: the DM's band is a ceiling over the derived one) has read `unfunded` for one season (DRAFT 13 weeks = `INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season`, UNMEASURED) → `abandoned`, holder cleared, `lastHolder` set. The dwell is DECLARED: this step sets `wantSince` the first tick the effective band reads `unfunded` and drops it the first tick it reads above (drop-when-absent; §11), so a keep garrisoned years ago and starved today abandons one season after the want began, never on the first unfunded read [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-16 · ST-r2-1: "A DM-set band never abandons" is STRUCK (RULINGS-ROUND2b)]. AND THE ONE WRITER DROPS `wantSince` AND `fundingSet` ON EVERY HOLDER CHANGE — `applySiteEvent` compares the holder's FULL IDENTITY `{ class, ref.kind, ref.bodyKind, ref.id, role, creed }` before and after each event and drops both whenever any part differs (`placed · garrisoned · taken · reclaimed · conveyed · ceded · granted · seized · consecrated · sold · cleared · abandoned · raised`, and a body's own take or leaving) — SAVE BD-0's ADOPTION, which fills an id-less authored gang's `ref.id` and is NO holder change (the same gang, its first record: an absent `ref.id` matches the id the adoption fills, §11's adoption row, S34) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 1: the carve-out lived in §11 and S34 only, so the writer as stated here would have dropped both at the adoption · A.2: `raised` listed] — so the want and the DM's ceiling are always the CURRENT holder's; and at the same comparison the writer SETS `holderSince` to the event's tick (drops it when the site falls unheld), the one stored tenure `holderSinceOf` reads [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-93)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.20 · ST-r2-16 (a)(b): Kell's garrison handed to Kell's own clergy (`ref` unchanged, `class` and `role` changed) IS a holder change; the ceiling no longer outlives its holder]. A BODY holder (gang or conclave) is EXEMPT from this step: its want is its supplies and its leaving is its own choice (THE BODIES §4; BD9: "Moving the base is a choice, never a drift"), so the step sets no `wantSince` for it [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — a conclave is a TIED `faith` holder, and the want clock would have walked a `strained` flock out of its shrine a season later by drift, against BD9]: a keep abandoned and re-garrisoned by the DM (`SITE_HOLDER`) starts a fresh clock, and a new holder that is still `unfunded` holds it one season, then walks away in the open (ST6 names the DM's real levers) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-16 (RULINGS-ROUND3 B): round 2 dropped the clock only "when it reads above", which an `abandoned` holder never does, so the unheld site kept a stale `wantSince` and the DM's re-garrison was abandoned again on the next tick].
4. **Dens and arrivals** (ST15): a FORCE arriving at an unheld site → `site_taken` — an untied force (a gang, a beast, a menace) or a CONCLAVE (a flock that takes an EMPTY site holds it as `faith`, L-BD 30: "can occupy a site"); at a held site → `site_siege` (a body or a menace attacking a site a settlement or a faith holds); a body moving on a site ANOTHER BODY holds is that site's `site_cleared` (BD §9.4: the clearer the body, the holder the other). The arrival's write returns the at-hand courts' SIGHTINGS (the might band seen at the tick), each written into the court's belief by MN's ONE door `recordSighting` (the one store, subject the arriver — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2), never onto the site (§2.4; A.17 · A.5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — B.2], and emits ONE `site_taken` news row (MN17's or THE BODIES' typed slots aboard), the telling every other court hears — UNLESS THE SITE IS `keptSecret`: then it emits NO news row and writes no sighting (A.16; §2.4's veil) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V1 · ST-V2] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.16 · ST-W3 · A.17 · ST-W2]. ONE hearing per ARRIVAL and ONE CONTEST per site per week, ONE SIDE AT A TIME (A.18 · A.3; step 6d draws it): the folded contest belongs to the AT-HAND courts — the ANCHOR court (`where.ids[0]`) contests first; the other at-hand court (a road's far end, `where.ids[1]`) that chooses to sortie JOINS the anchor's sortie as ONE force (their FIELD FORCES summed — `homeDefense − siteDefense` each, the ready keep staying at its walls, A.1 · A.5; the clearer named is the anchor, the joiner receipted); if the anchor does not sortie, the other contests alone; only the ANCHOR'S SIDE joins — a second hostile force arriving the same week is its own challenger side, and the STRONGER arrival is this week's (step 6d) — never two contests and never a second `site_cleared` for the same arrival [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.18 · ST-W4 (MAJOR): two at-hand courts both saw the arrival and both could sortie on a hearing with one clearer and one draw] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3 · ST-X1 (V1 and V2): r4's widening summed every mover — a rival gang, a menace — into the court's force]. A court sorties only against an arriver it holds HOSTILE — a menace; a beast (every court hunts beasts, BD10); a gang it holds `hunted`; a conclave it holds `outlawed`; an id-less authored gang — never against a `permitted`, `curbed` or `endowed` flock (L-BD 30: "may not be pursued by settlement"), nor a gang it `tolerated`s, `employed`s or `harboured`s (THE BODIES' one stance read `courtStanceOf`, BD §7.1; with THE BODIES dark every untied arriver is hostile, as before) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (3) · L-BD 30]; a court attached only `nearby` or `distant` cannot know the arrival in its week (it hears it later, by the telling) and answers by step 6's `site_cleared` from the next week [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-17] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V1: the folded contest now has a court that can reach it]. The arrivals are produced by their owners: a BODY's own weekly choice (THE BODIES §4 — a gang's `move` seeking an empty den, a pack's lair, a flock seeking a holy place: THE BODIES' stage, the leaf after ST's and PR's in the same head, calls ST's arrival door `siteStage.js :: arriveAt(worldState, siteId, arriverRef, tick)`, which only REGISTERS that arrival for the week's one contest (the body's place on the site is NOT yet written), drawn by ST's closing call after THE BODIES' leaf (step 6d), whose per-mover verdict `arrived · held` the body's own door applies in the same chain (RULINGS-ROUND6 B.6) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-Y1: "`arriveAt` runs this step … and REGISTERS it" said both] — a closed caller; WC-12's located unit IS THE BODIES' gang record, BD §19 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route: "WC-12's located unit … (unbuilt — the slot)" filled by THE BODIES]), a menace whose DM-set course is `toward <site>` or `besieges <site>` (MN's `MENACE_COURSES` gain a site target when ST lands — MN's additive row), a DM verb.
5. **Reclaim offers** (ST13) — THE OFFER READS BELIEF (A.4): for a site that FELL UNTIED (a force took it), `site_reclaim` is minted for its standing `lastHolder` court in the FIRST WEEK that court BELIEVES the force holds it (`believedDenOf` known — an at-hand court in the arrival's own week by its sighting, a farther court the week the telling reaches it) and only while that BELIEF stands (a reclaim decided on a belief the force has since outrun meets the truth at the site, §2.4) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify A.5's third truth gate: "and only while the force still holds it" read truth]; an UNHEARD den, or a `keptSecret` one the court has not witnessed, is never offered — no reclaim, no clearing, no `den_unanswered` until it is heard or seen (OD-ST-35 and A.16 extended to the reclaim). For a site that FELL UNHELD, the offer is minted in the week it falls when the court's OWN act emptied it (its garrison abandoned it) and, when a FORCE's leaving or end emptied it, in the week the court holds the unmaking telling (the at-hand courts from the next tick, §2.4) [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — Law One carried to the unheld arm: a court never answers a departure it has not heard of]. A site that changes from one force to another — a promotion's re-point (§6.7), a won clearing by a force (step 6d) — never falls empty or newly untied, so it mints no offer [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.4 · the ruling's ST-X2 (V1): the offer was minted on TRUTH, so for a den the court had never heard of a `lavish` court's `reclaimScore` read 3 − 2 + 0 = 1 → `moves`, and `moves` minted `site_cleared` unconditionally — a kept-secret shrine's last clergy court cleared a gang it could not know, against A.16; with the offer gated on belief that arithmetic is never reached].
6. **Clearings (an UNTIED holder):** an attached tied court whose town the den presses (`denPressOf` on that town ≥ 0.40, DRAFT — the one presence term the composer carries for that holder, ST's or THE BODIES', §2.4; a fact of its own land, felt on its own roads), which holds the den's holder HOSTILE (as step 4 defines it; a CONCLAVE's site is never this step's — a tied holder presses nothing, so its site is SEIZED by the court's stance, step 6c [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1 · L-BD 30 (V2 fail): this gate made the seizure unreachable]) and which BELIEVES it can contest the den (`believedDenOf`, §2.4: its FIELD FORCE `homeDefense − siteDefense` from the capacity lookup — the ready keep stays at its walls, A.1 · A.5 — against the den's defender AS IT BELIEVES IT, the holder's might seen or heard plus the site's walls (§8), ratio ≥ 1.2, DRAFT; truth only in the omniscient arm; a `keptSecret` den the court has not witnessed, or an unheard one, is never answered) SORTIES through its own `clearingFork` — the court's landed risk posture (`strategicPosture.js`, requirement 10) as the one bounded signal — → `site_cleared` at that week's climax, for a den ALREADY held before this week (an arrival this week is `site_taken`'s contest) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-15 · ST-r2-17] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.5 · A.18 · BD's route (2), (3): the sortie's force is the field force (round 3 read `offensive`, which counts an army marched elsewhere); the press reads the one term the composer carries]. ONE CONTEST PER SITE PER WEEK, ONE SIDE AT A TIME (A.18 · A.3): the ANCHOR court leads when it sorties; every other court that sorties on the same den that week JOINS it as ONE force (field forces summed; each joiner receipted); when the anchor does not sortie, the sortieing court nearest by band leads (codepoint tie-break); a body or a menace moving on the same den that week is a separate challenger unless the leading court harbours or employs it — step 6d draws the week's one contest and names who holds after; never two contests. A reclaim's `moves` against an untied holder mints `site_cleared` at the NEXT week's climax (the docket resolves the reclaim first, and nothing it decides opens a second hearing in its own week — requirement 24(b)); a grouped patrol (THE PATROLS) is its court's sortie and joins that week's one contest at its band; a BODY may clear another body's site (BD §9.4); the party clears through PT's ONE door (§7.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-17 · ST-r2-19].
6b. **The forced sale** (L-BD 30: a non-hostile conclave "may not be pursued by settlement but may be forced to give it up in exchange for resources"): an attached court at `at_hand` that holds the site's conclave's creed `permitted` or `curbed` (never `endowed` — it keeps the flock; never `outlawed` — it seizes, step 6c [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1]), whose effective band is `funded` or better (it can pay), DEMANDS the site through its `saleFork` (no randomness) where the site is its own use — a `fortification` or `watchtower` at hand (its defence) or a site it last held (`lastHolder`) — at most once per court and site a year (DRAFT, UNMEASURED), and ONE demand per site per week (the anchor's first, A.18's rule) → `site_sale` at that week's climax (§8) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the producer's bounds (an at-hand court, `funded` or better, a site of its own use, once a year, one demand a week) are the lane's, so the sale never becomes a weekly lever on every flock]: the flock answers by its god's recorded axes, the price is paid in the flock's supplies, a refusal hardens the court's stance one step [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — L-BD 30 · RULINGS-ROUND4 B (ST) — measured: ST's conveyance road is WR-10's court-to-court hearing ("Every holding a campaign court could convey"), whose conveyor must be a campaign court, so a body cannot sell through it; ST names its own kind `site_sale` (FREE, `D-ST-r4-probe/census-r4.out`), whose `sold` is already one of ST's own `SITE_EVENTS` and whose `refused` carries the conveyance row's own meaning — a holder declines to give up a holding].
6c. **The seizure — by STANCE, never by press** (A.1; L-BD 30: "a SEIZURE instead of a sale is the court's choice and makes the conclave hostile"): a court attached `at_hand` to a site a CONCLAVE holds, which holds the flock's creed `curbed` or `outlawed` — NEVER `permitted` (the sale alone, step 6b) and never `endowed` (the patron keeps its flock) — and which BELIEVES it can take the site (`believedDenOf` → THE BODIES' `believedBodyOf`: its FIELD FORCE against the flock's believed strength plus the site's walls, ratio ≥ 1.2 — step 6's test; a `keptSecret` site it has not witnessed is never seized), SEIZES it through its `clearingFork` (the court's risk posture, requirement 10): under `outlawed`, whenever the fork moves (the flock is already the court's enemy); under `curbed`, only after the flock has REFUSED that court's own demand (`site_sale` `refused`, step 6b) within the year — the price offered before the sword, the owner's "forced to give it up in exchange for resources" kept as the first road; a court that CANNOT make the demand (below `funded`, or the site of no use to it — 6b's bounds) seizes a curbed flock's site only by first OUTLAWING the creed (its own stance act, BD §7.1), so the seizure is never unreachable, only dearer [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify note 1, said so] [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the ruling allows the seizure under `curbed`; the lane orders it after a refused sale, so a curbing court never seizes a flock it never offered to pay — vetoable]. The seizure IS `site_cleared` at that week's climax (the court the clearer, the flock the holder; the defender the flock's `bodyStrength` plus the site's walls, §8), drawn as the week's one contest (step 6d); its consequence is L-BD 30's: the flock is HOSTILE from that week — a `curbed` court that seizes turns the creed `outlawed` through THE BODIES' `body_stance` writer (ROUTED to BD: BD §7.1's and §9.4's seizure lines cite this step, not step 6) — and an outlawed flock may raid and take captives (B.2). Step 6's press test is NOT read: a tied holder has no hazard term (§3.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1 · L-BD 30 (V2's fail, in part): the seizure was gated on `denPressOf`, which is 0 for every conclave-held site (ST's feeder covers untied sites, THE BODIES' handoff untied body holders), so an outlawing court had no road to its own keep once a flock held it].

6d. **THE WEEK'S ONE CONTEST PER SITE — ONE SIDE AT A TIME** (A.3 · ST-X1, V1 and V2). Steps 4, 6, 6c and 7, a grouped patrol (PR) and THE BODIES' `arriveAt` REGISTER every move on a site this week; the contests are DRAWN ONCE per site — at the docket's resolution where `hearingWeekEnabled` is lit, else by ST's CLOSING CALL `siteStage.js :: closeSiteWeek`, the leaf after THE BODIES' in the same head (+1 host line in `advanceCalamity`, ST-2) [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the closing call: THE BODIES' stage runs after ST's, so without it a body's arrival could not be weighed against a court's sortie of the same tick]. THE SIDES: THE ANCHOR'S SIDE is the tied courts that sortie or seize (the ANCHOR court leading, `where.ids[0]`; when it stays home, the sortieing court nearest by band, codepoint tie-break), their FIELD FORCES summed (`homeDefense − siteDefense` each, A.5), a grouped patrol at its court's band, and any BODY the ANCHOR'S stance (the leading court's, when the anchor stays home) marks `harboured` or `employed` that moves on the site this week (its `bodyStrength` added); EVERY OTHER mover is its own CHALLENGER side — a body that court does not so mark (a rival gang, a `hunted` one, a beast, a flock of any creed), a menace, an army through ST-2b. THE ONE CONTEST: where the site is HELD, the STRONGEST challenger side (its force on the latent scale — TRUTH, for the draw resolves the physics, CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d); codepoint tie-break on its leader) contests the holder; where it is UNHELD, the strongest arriving force takes it, contested by the anchor's side if it sorties (`site_taken`'s folded contest); a holder never fights two sides in one week. THE OTHERS HOLD AT THE APPROACH: every other challenger side has NOT ARRIVED this week — every held challenger is held by PS's ONE hold helper in its FREEZE mode as THE SITES' approach registrant, AT ITS PRIOR POSITION (PS §4.1's order — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.6): an army in transit by the helper's two clocks; a body or a menace (no transit record) by the same FREEZE, which for a clockless record is the WITHHELD COMMIT — its mover REGISTERED the move on the site (`arriveAt` for a body, MN's mover for a menace) without writing its place onto the site, so its record still stands at the approach and nothing is rolled back; ST makes no claim of its own here, and the WINNING arrival is committed by its OWN owner's door (THE BODIES' writer through `arriveAt`'s commit, MN's mover) in the same chain, from this call's per-mover verdict `arrived · held` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify ST-Y1 (MAJOR: the closing call ran after both movers had already moved, so a held challenger had two truths of its place for a week or a rollback no volume named) · RULINGS-ROUND6 B.6: ST-Y1's option (a), the move a REQUEST; `arriveAt` only registers], a court's sortie waits (its fork re-decides next week against whoever then holds) — and, if its own choice still moves it on the site next week, it ARRIVES then and contests whoever then holds: a fresh hearing in its own week, never a queued one (requirement 24: nothing queues); its hold this week is a receipt on the week's contest card ("… while the Red Hand waits below the keep"). ONE HEARING PER ARRIVAL, ONE CONTEST PER SITE PER WEEK — the two reconciled: each arrival is heard once, in the week it arrives; only the week's one contest draws (stream the kind's own key, §8). ONE HOLDER CHANGE PER SITE PER WEEK: a `site_sale` that closes `sold` this week is the week's holder change (the conveyance is resolved first, the holder's own act), and every challenger side that week holds at the approach (above) and arrives next week against the BUYER [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify note 4: a `sold` sale and a drawn contest could each change the holder in one week, unordered; vetoable]. THE HOLDER AFTER A WON CONTEST IS THE CLEARER NAMED (A.3): a won `site_cleared` drives the holder out (`cleared`) and puts the LEADER of the winning side in the site IN THE SAME ACT — a court as its kind wants (§2.1: a shrine its clergy, every other kind its garrison — `garrisoned`, or `reclaimed` where it is the `lastHolder`), an army's settlement the same, a body as itself (a gang or a beast `untied`, a flock as `faith`), a menace as itself (a lair) — so the one writer's full-identity comparison sees ONE holder change and the sightings of a force holder are returned (§2.4); the joiners are receipted and hold nothing [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3 rules the JOINT `cleared`; the same holder rule is applied to a SOLO one, so a court's won clearing never leaves the site it won to a stranger and a body's never differs from a court's; PR R11's "on `cleared` the site is unheld and ST's reclaim follows" is ROUTED to PR]; a site_taken `driven_off` leaves the site as it was (unheld — nothing was taken from anyone); the party's clearing through PT's door (no contest) leaves it unheld (the party holds nothing — OD-ST-42 ⭐ is the owner's) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3 · ST-X1: round 4's "whatever courts, patrols or bodies move on one site in one week meet it in ONE hearing with one draw" summed a hunted gang, a rival gang or a menace into the court's force, named no leader for a body and a menace with no court, and left the holder after a mixed `cleared` undefined].

7. **Standing sieges:** each `site_siege` resolves its week (§8), its draw the site's one contest of the week (step 6d).
8. **THE CRISIS SPENDS** (ST11; §3.5's last column) [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-10 (WC-ST-41; RULINGS-P5 C.10)]. THE KEEP'S SPEND (§4.2) IS THE TEMPLATE: in its function's crisis a HELD site is spent before the town — ONE quality step per crisis (`sound → worn → ruined`, or `destroyed` where the row says so), a dated event through ST's one writer with `causedBy` the crisis's own id and the receipt naming it, IDEMPOTENT by that id (a crisis spends a site once; PLAUSIBLE that the eight-event ring always holds it for the crisis's life — ST-3's car-0 pins it on a ring-eviction fixture, else the writer keys the spend on the site's `lastTick` and the crisis id); a site unheld when the crisis strikes is not spent (it has no function to spend); a relieved or lifted crisis leaves the site as it stands, and funding restores it over years (step 2). THE SIX READS, each read TRUTH (the crisis is the town's own state) and each lit by `siteEffectsEnabled` AND its source's own flag (dark source ⇒ no crisis ⇒ nothing):
   - (a) **the hospice** — an outbreak stressor on a town the hospice is attached to `at_hand` reaches its PEAK: the town's `worldState.stressors` row at the landed stage `peaking` (`stressorsCore.js :: STRESSOR_LIFECYCLE_STAGES`: `emerging · active · peaking · easing · resolved · residual`) → `worn` (its capacity overwhelmed), `causedBy` the stressor's id;
   - (b) **the shrine** — a `religious_conversion_fracture` on its town RESOLVES (its row reads `residual`, the landed echo) with the seated creed no longer the shrine's creed (its `faith` holder's `creed`, else its `deity` tag) → `worn`; and a shrine TAKEN by an untied force (a gang, a beast, a menace) at this week's contest (step 4, committed at step 6d) → `destroyed`, quality `ruined`;
   - (c) **the tower — THE WARDS** — a `magic_deadzone` or `magical_instability` stressor on its town resolves (`residual`) while it is held → `worn`;
   - (d) **the watchtower — THE BLINDED WATCH** — this week's contest commits an untied force's TAKE of a site whose place the tower watches (`siteWitnesses(worldState, place)` names the tower's holder) and the take returns NO sighting for the tower's holder (a `keptSecret` site — the veil, §2.4) → `worn` (the watch saw nothing);
   - (e) **the crossing — THE CUT** — an enemy ARMY's `site_siege` on a held `crossing` (ST-2b) that the week's one contest decides `falls` → `destroyed`, quality `ruined`, the holder after the army's settlement (step 6d: it holds a cut bridge, restored only by funding) — lit by `siteWarTargetEnabled`; the DM's `SITE_STATUS` cuts one too [REFINED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — "when an enemy army cuts it" had no act: the cut IS the army's won siege of the bridge; vetoable];
   - (f) **the harbour works** — a blockade holds its port (`spatial/navalLayer.js :: activeBlockadeTargets(worldState)` names the anchor) for DRAFT 13 weeks (UNMEASURED; the blockade's age read off its naval transit record — `navalTransitLedger(worldState)`'s `BLOCKADE` row for this target: `planBlockade` stamps `departTick: depart, arrivalTick: depart + weeks`, so the age ON STATION is `max(0, tick − arrivalTick)`, CONFIRMED at master `spatial/navalLayer.js :: planBlockade`; `activeBlockadeTargets` lists a blockade from its departure, so the arm reads `arrivalTick`, never the listing's first week) [REFINED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — the field named (V-ALL-p5's P5C-10 note: "master `planBlockade` sets `departTick`/`arrivalTick`, so read `arrivalTick`"); the former "PLAUSIBLE until ST-3's car-0 names the field" closed] → `worn` once per blockade, `causedBy` the blockade record's key — lit by `navalEnabled`.
   It runs at the CLOSING CALL `closeSiteWeek`, after the week's contests are drawn (so (b), (d) and (e) read the takes and falls as committed; where `hearingWeekEnabled` is lit, at the docket's resolution, the same order); zero host lines beyond the closing call's. **The cards:** "The hospice at Ashford is spent; the sickness filled it past bearing." · "The bridge at Larkford is cut." — the receipt names the crisis (the Evidence Law).

**Clock** (requirement 9): weekly steps; wear and restoration yearly; abandonment after a season; reclaim and clearing in the week. **Kernel order:** the danger step (`advanceEmbattlement`, kernel ~2391) runs BEFORE the calamity stage (~2527), so a site change is felt on the roads the following tick (read-last, write-next; stated, not a defect). Inside `advanceCalamity`'s head the order is MN's stage → ST's stage (steps 1–7, registering the week's moves) → PR's → THE BODIES' (its `arriveAt` calls) → ST's CLOSING CALL `closeSiteWeek` (step 6d: each site's one contest, where the pause is dark; it returns `arrived · held` per registered mover and the movers' owners commit or withhold in the same chain — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.6; MN's same-tick hold reads the same), whose last act is step 8, THE CRISIS SPENDS [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-Y1 · B.6] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-10].

### 6.2 The DM's hand — five realm verbs (each ≤ four dials; a realm-manifest row; a proposal whose `targetSaveId` is the site's ANCHOR settlement with the site on a `subject` dial, `anchor␟siteId` — MN9's convention; AUTHOR acts provenance-marked as authorship; premium)

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.14 (measured; nothing to replace): the `␟` in `anchor␟siteId` and in `PLACE_SITE`'s road value `a␟b` is the realm manifest's LANDED composite-target DIAL separator (`events/realmManifest.js :: COURSE_TARGET_SEP = '␟'`, parsed as "`targetId: raw.slice(0, i), courseKey: raw.slice(i + COURSE_TARGET_SEP.length)`"), a dial value read once at the verb, never a stored key; the stored road is `where.ids [a, b]`, and wherever a STRETCH KEY is needed it is the digest's landed `pairKey` form (`spatialDigest.js :: pairKey`, which joins the codepoint-sorted pair with a vertical bar), `stretch:<a|b>` (A.14). ST mints no `a␟b` key.]

| Verb | Hat | Dials | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PLACE_SITE` | AUTHOR | `kind` (enum) · `name` (text) · `where` (target: a settlement = its country, or `a␟b` = the road) · `holder` (enum: none · the anchor's garrison · the anchor's civil hold · the anchor's clergy — a person, a body or (before THE BODIES) an authored gang, which need an id or a might, is set afterwards by `SITE_HOLDER`, whose dials carry them) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-30 (1)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3: "a band" → a body or an id-less authored gang] | mints the record (`placed`; or `adopted` when it comes through the map's offer — its `offerRef`, its `mapSiteRef` (the join key) and the map's placement as `where.position`, ENC-HL-03; or `ruin_left` from a dying settlement's card); quality `sound` (a `ruin` `ruined`); a shrine takes the anchor creed's key as its `deity` unless the DM picks another, and is `keptSecret` by default (court knowledge; J384 (10)'s DM-only default is the audience rule, ST-4). THE ADOPT ARM IS H28's "host command adopting a DM-placed holy or hidden cult site" (the host's "cult site" is a creed's small flock's holy place — the faith ladder's `cult` standing, never villainy, A.3) — ONE writer, this one (C15, waiting on V14 Q2 / ST-0), never a second command in WF's faith receipts [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-7 (3) · HOST-r2-10 · HOST-r2-19] |
| `SITE_HOLDER` | ORDER (AUTHOR when `hat` = `author`) | `subject` · `holder` (target/enum: a court's garrison or clergy, a menace, a body by its ref, or an id-less authored gang) · `might` (enum, an id-less authored gang only — before THE BODIES; once they are lit a gang or a flock is placed by THE BODIES' `PLACE_BODY` and set here by its ref, BD §11) · `hat` (`order · author`) | an ORDER goes through the world's grammar (a court's garrison moves in = `garrisoned`; a gang is set down = `taken`); an AUTHOR act "is so" — and an AUTHORED FORCE holder (a menace, a body, an authored gang) is told like any other: the one writer returns the at-hand courts' sightings — written into MN's ONE sighting store by `recordSighting` (subject the holder) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2: one store, one door], none for an id-less gang, which is believed by the telling alone (§2.4; A.5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5] — and EMITS the same `site_taken` news row with cause `dm` (no hearing: the DM's act is the verdict) — one writer, one kind, so a DM-placed den (S4) is believed, cleared and answered like a den that arrived — EXCEPT at a `keptSecret` site, where the authored placement emits NOTHING until `made_known` (A.16) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V2] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · A.16 · ST-W3] |
| `SITE_STATUS` | AUTHOR | `subject` · `event` (enum: `abandoned · destroyed · restored · consecrated · made_known · kept_secret`) · `funding` (enum: a band, or `derived`) · `quality` (enum, or `as is`) | the DM's dated events and the funding CEILING (ST6); a quality set writes one `worn` / `restored` per band moved (or `destroyed`); `made_known` lifts the court veil and, where a force holds the site, the one writer THEN emits its telling (the `site_taken` row, cause `dm`) and returns the at-hand courts' sightings for MN's one door `recordSighting` (A.5 · B.2) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2: one store, one door] — the first telling a kept-secret den ever makes (A.16) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-23 · HOST-r2-19] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.16] |
| `END_SITE` | AUTHOR | `subject` · `reason` (enum: `gone · razed`) | writes `ended`, a chronicle line, and drops the record (lived history stays in the chronicle; undo restores it) |
| `SITE_KIND` | AUTHOR | `subject` · `kind` (enum) · `name` (text) | writes `rekinded` (the prior kind in `from`) and KEEPS the id, so the map's joined document re-kinds instead of a second site standing beside a stale one (a ruin rebuilt into a keep) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-8 (3): END + PLACE minted a second map site] |

The list is now the realm verb arm, ST's stage and its closing call, the war layer's keep leaf, GRAMMAR's peace-term executor, PL's charter and verdict leaves, WR-10's conveyance arm, PT's clearing door, and THE BODIES' ledger and stage — whose ledger, writing a body's `raised` end at the DM's promotion, re-points the site's holder in the same act (§6.7, A.2).]

### 6.4 The last to act has the first claim (ST13; ruling 10)

`site_reclaim` is offered to `lastHolder`'s court in the FIRST WEEK THAT COURT BELIEVES the site fallen empty or untied (§6.1 step 5 — the week of the fall itself only where the court's own act emptied it or it sees the fall at hand) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify A.4 residue: the r5 sentence opened on truth] (A.4; a court far from its old keep may find a nearer court has moved first: the preference is the believer's, never a claim on truth) — BEFORE any other court's clearing or garrisoning may move on it that week [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.4]. Its DEFAULT (the court's own fork, which draws no randomness): `reclaimScore = fundingRank (unfunded 0 · meagre 1 · funded 2 · lavish 3) + (against an untied holder) the believed force ratio's term (§6.1 step 6: ≥ 1.2 → +1, else −2) + the court's risk-posture term (requirement 10, ±1)`, and the court `moves` when it stands and `reclaimScore ≥ 1` (DRAFT, UNMEASURED); else `declines`. **The tilt** (requirement 24(b): "a declared bounded term on the decider's score where the decider draws no randomness"): ±1 slight · ±2 heavy on `reclaimScore` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-18: every kind offers the three choices]. `moves` on an unheld site = `reclaimed` + `garrisoned` (a shrine to its clergy, §2.1); on an untied one = the `site_cleared` contest at the NEXT week's climax (§6.1 step 6), which, won, puts the reclaimer back in the site in the same act (`cleared` + `reclaimed` — the clearer named holds, step 6d). THE OFFER READS BELIEF (A.4, §6.1 step 5): it is made in the first week the court believes the site fallen to a force (or holds the unmaking telling of one that left), never on a den it has not heard of or seen; the believed force ratio's term reads that belief [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.4 · A.3]. A decline (or a court that cannot) opens the site to whoever acts next: the attached courts by distance band (nearest first, codepoint tie-break), the party by deed. A preference, never a right — nothing is forbidden, and the DM may decide any week.

### 6.5 A site outlives its town (the owner's third word; ruling 13; ST14)

A settlement founded over a site (ENC-HP-01: the map's placement folds the site in; or the DM's founding) HOLDS it like any holder: the site's anchor and holder name the settlement (the site carries the reference; no settlement field is added — one reference direction). When that settlement fails by ANY lifecycle path — terminal decline, the DM's `forceAbandonSettlement`, a razing, a world imported without it, a regeneration that does not re-create it — §6.1 step 1 leaves the site standing: holder none, `abandoned`, quality unchanged then wearing, attachments re-derived to the surviving neighbours, the first claim lapsed. The dying settlement's card OFFERS "leave a ruin" (`PLACE_SITE`, kind `ruin`, `placedBy: 'ruin_left'`, the dead settlement as `lastHolder` for the chronicle only) — the DM's act; the engine never spawns one. THE CARD'S HOST, named at ST-0's car-0 from the candidates at master: (1) `components/map/HeraldRemembrance.jsx` — "RUINS & REMEMBRANCE, the Herald's graveyard door", whose rows already carry "the terminal-death lane's relic ruins and abandoned sites" for EVERY death path; the offer is a link that opens `PLACE_SITE`'s composer prefilled (kind `ruin`, `placedBy: 'ruin_left'`), so the door itself still "writes nothing" and the act stays the DM's approved verb — THE RECOMMENDATION; (2) the `FORCE_ABANDON` verb's approval card (`realmVerbExecution.js`'s `case 'FORCE_ABANDON'`), which covers the DM's abandonment alone. Car-0 measures the door's lines and picks ONE (a UI leaf, one link) [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-64; RULINGS-P5 C.17: "the ruin card's host named by ST at its car-0 (candidates at master)")]. **The map's side** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-8]: at a settlement's founding over a site the map RETIRES its site document ("its levels become the settlement's; the site document is retired; a dying settlement keeps its sites", ENC-MS-06) and keeps its id; this record keeps `mapSiteRef`, so when that settlement fails and §6.1 step 1 leaves the site standing, the fold's map INBOX asks the map to RE-EMIT its document under the same id (a cross-program ask, the chair's ODQ row — the fold's map INBOX row (j) THE SITE JOIN KEY, which already carries the ask ("when that settlement dies (ST14) the map is asked to RE-EMIT its site document for the same id") [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-65): the ask had no row among the map INBOX's (a)–(m)] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — RULINGS-P5 D.2 · D.6: the P5 bracket's "(n)" was wrong and the fold lane REFUTED it — the ask has stood since r2b in row (j) (HOST-r2-8, ST's round-2b cure); (n) is THE HIDDEN WAYS; the cite re-pointed to (j), nothing new owed to the map]) — the host and the map hold one joined site. WHILE THE FOUNDING SETTLEMENT LIVES, the two COEXIST: the host's row stands (its holder the settlement) while the map's site document is retired — ENC-MS-06 retires it at the founding ("its levels become the settlement's; the site document is retired; a dying settlement keeps its sites", HR:694) — and `mapSiteRef` keeps the two joined across the retirement, so nothing is lost and nothing stands twice [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — note 4: round 2's "never a live host row over a retired map document" was false for as long as the founding settlement lives].

### 6.6 A den that presses a member (ruling 12 — the legible consequence)

THE OCCASION AND ITS TWO CLOSINGS (A.6) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.6 · SEAMS-r3-5 (MAJOR): round 3's occasion wrote `protection_failed` and nothing ever wrote `protection_kept` for a den the liege answered (ST: 0 hits), so the hold's `P` could only fall from it]. The occasion `den_unanswered` OPENS on a SWORN member's town the first week the den presses it at or above 0.40 (`denPressOf`, DRAFT) AND the liege could know it (the liege's `believedDenOf` holds the den; a liege is never blamed for a den it has not heard of) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-15], for a den the MEMBER holds hostile (§6.1 step 4's test — a den the member itself `tolerated`s, `employed`s or `harboured`s is the member's own choice, priced by PL's harbouring row where the liege hunts it, BD §7.1 → PL, never the liege's failure) and never for a `keptSecret` den (A.16 · OD-ST-35); its CLOCK is one season (DRAFT 13 weeks, UNMEASURED). It CLOSES ONCE, under its one episode `den_unanswered:<siteId>:<tick>` (`<tick>` the opening tick; PL's door refuses a second outcome for the same edge and episode, PL §4.6 — D-PL-r4's route, cited): `protection_kept` when the den is CLEARED within the clock — by the liege's answer (its own sortie if it is attached, its army through ST-2b, its riders, or a decided relief PL already owns) or by anyone (the member, a third court, a body, the party) — AND the site then stands unheld or held by a holder the member does NOT hold hostile (a court's garrison, a flock it permits): a hostile force that clears a den and keeps it (a rival gang, a menace — the clearer now holds, §6.1 step 6d) leaves a den, so the occasion runs on [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V1 note 3: A.6's "cleared by anyone" read as the DEN gone, never one robber band for another; routed to PL's mirror wording] — the member stood; `protection_failed` when the clock EXPIRES with the den still holding — the member was not protected, the receipt saying whether the liege came and was repelled ("Kell's lord came, but the robbers held the keep") or never came (A.1's principle: the outcome by harm, the answer on the receipt); and NO row when the den ends or leaves within the clock by its own course (a menace's end, a body's end or move — no protection was tested) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.6 names the cleared and the unanswered closings; the repelled liege and the self-ending den are stated so every opened occasion closes exactly once]. ST supplies the occasion and PL records the outcome row on the sworn edge through its request door (`requestFealtyRow`, episode `den_unanswered:<siteId>:<tick>`, a registered `FEALTY_EPISODE_PREFIXES` member — PL §4) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PL's request door (SEAMS-r2-10 is PL's) named for ST's occasion] — PL's one writer, the occasion registered in PL's own commit (the SEAMS-r1-2 registration idiom) — a row the crown's hold reads through its `P` (PL P43: "H's `P` falls by it" for `protection_failed`; its mirror rises by `protection_kept` — ROUTED to PL) and nothing else; BOTH closings ride the SAME flags (requested under `siteFatesEnabled` ∧ `polityEnabled`, A.5), so the den's two outcomes are symmetric under every legal flag set and never wait on `polityObligationsEnabled` (SEAMS-r3-5's one-way case closed on ST's side): the hold's `P` moves by OUTCOME and the request door patches trust only for `fealty_defied` (A.6), so the row carries no trust or resentment patch of its own; the occasion is requested under `siteFatesEnabled` ∧ `polityEnabled` (A.5: a requested row rides the requester's flag ∧ `polityEnabled`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.5 · A.6: round 2's "carrying the member's resentment patch `protection_failed` already carries (PL §4.3, P9)" is narrowed to the truth A.6 rules]. So a crown that lets its border forts rot loses their protection AND makes dens that press its own members, and its hold frays by the protection record it already reads — and a crown whose riders clear the dens its members suffer is credited with the protection it gave, by the same record. The clock: weekly check from the opening, one season to close; the receipt names the den, the liege and the closing.

### 6.7 Who makes a den (the owner's ninth word; ruling 23; ST15)

| The untied holder | Who brings it | Emergent or the DM's |
|---|---|---|
| a GANG (THE BODIES) | its own weekly choice — a `move` seeking an empty den, a base taken by force (BD §4) — or BD-0's adoption of an authored gang; WC-12's located raiders ARE THE BODIES' gang records (until C17 lands, engine-born dens do not occur) | EMERGENT — the world's own, born from a fact the world holds (BD2) |
| a BEAST (THE BODIES) | its own choice — a lair in cover country, bred by season (BD §3.2, §6.3); never placed by the DM (a pack the DM names and drives is a MENACE, BD10) | EMERGENT — the world's own |
| a menace | the DM's course (`toward` / `besieges` a site), or `SITE_HOLDER`, or the DM's PROMOTION of the body that held the site (it holds it in place, A.2) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2] | THE DM'S ALONE — never organically emergent (MN12) |
| a DM-authored gang | THE BODIES' `PLACE_BODY` then `SITE_HOLDER` by its ref; before C17, `SITE_HOLDER` with `might` (an id-less gang, §2.1) | THE DM'S |
| a villain paragon | its own weekly choice (CH §4.4's weights gain a site row: a post, a tower, a lair) — the paragon exists only because the DM introduced it | EMERGENT CHOICE of a DM-introduced person; its purpose only by the DM's direction |

A CONCLAVE MAKES NO DEN (A.3): a flock that takes a site — by arrival at an empty one, or by clearing a hostile body's (L-BD 30) — holds it as `faith`, a church's site whose sign in each town follows that town's stance toward its creed (§3.1); a court that wants it back may DEMAND it against resources (`site_sale`, §6.1 step 6b) or SEIZE it by its stance — holding the creed `outlawed`, or `curbed` once the flock has refused its demand (step 6c; A.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 · SEAMS-r3-1 (BLOCKING) · ST-W1: the rows "a cult | the faith system's own heresy" and "a DM-authored band or cult" STRUCK; the brigand row filled by THE BODIES (BD's route (1), (4)).]

Promotion of an ambient gang's den into a named menace is the DM's act (THE BODIES' `PROMOTE_BODY` staging MN's `RAISE_MENACE` with the body's place and might and the DM's name — MN §2, MN12's adoption road, BD16), never the engine's. ON PROMOTION THE SITE'S HOLDER BECOMES THE MENACE (A.2 — MN's reading, MN §2 and M59): the DM promoted the body IN PLACE, so its den is now the menace's camp. In the promotion's OWN act (the approval of the staged `RAISE_MENACE`), THE BODIES' ledger, writing the body's `raised` end, calls ST's one writer (already a closed caller, ST2) with the new menace's id (derived from the raise's outcome id, MN §2), and the writer RE-POINTS the holder ref `{ kind: 'body', bodyKind, id }` → `{ kind: 'menace', id }` — ONE dated event `raised` (§2.3). The site is NOT cleared, NO unmaking telling is emitted (the world hears of the menace by MN's own raise news, MN17), NO reclaim is offered (the site never falls empty or newly untied, §6.1 step 5), and ST writes no sighting (the courts' belief of the gang stands as it was, stale, until MN's tellings of the menace reach them — A.5); the full-identity comparison counts it a holder change (`ref.kind` body → menace), so `fundingSet` drops and the band is the menace's (`unfunded`: its keep only wears), `wantSince` moot (an untied holder sets none); the den's presence hands over the same tick (THE BODIES' row stops, the menace's starts — one term per thing, MN §2). BD §9.4 and §15 align: a `raised` end HANDS THE SITE OVER, never empties it (ROUTED to BD) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2 · RU-MN-r4 (V2's ROUTED-UNAPPLIED fail; the ruling's RU-MN-1): round 4 emptied the site on `raised` ("the new menace holds it only where the DM's course or `SITE_HOLDER` puts it there"), against MN M59's "the ruin's holder ref reads `{ kind: 'menace', id }` (ST's writer)" — the DM's new menace lost its lair in the tick it was raised, the anchor was told the den was gone and `lastHolder` was offered a reclaim; ST's choice of the route's two arms: THE BODIES' `raised` end calls the writer (no new caller)].

## §7 SITES CONSIDERED EVERYWHERE (the owner's eighth word; ruling 20; ST18)

### 7.1 BY CONSTRUCTION — the coverage census (every decider that reads these numbers considers sites with no wiring of its own)

| The one read (ST's seam) | Its consumers (`path :: symbol`) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|

### 7.2 BY ONE NAMED READ — where a decider must know a site AS a site

| Decider | The read | Status |
|---|---|---|
| THE WAR CHOOSER — target a den or a fort | `sitesNear` + the deployer's `site` target arm (ST-2b: a deployment record gains `siteTargetId`; the siege loop resolves `site_siege` through ST's resolver instead of dropping the target — `warDeployment.js` today: "`if (!snapshot?.byId?.has?.(targetId)) continue;`") | a CAR (ST-2b), measured at its car-0 |
| THE PASSAGE planner — forts on the path as sieges to take or bypass | `sitesNear` on each node of the path; the fort's siege weeks in the believed cost (PS item 5) | PS's wave cites it |
| THE CALL BOARD — dens as calls | CH §14's `wildland` domain gains a source row: an untied-held site attached to a settlement, `side: 'help'`, known by presence, arrival or belief like every call | CH's commit registers the row |
| THE MENACE'S course — aim at a site | `MENACE_COURSES` `toward <site>` / `besieges <site>` (MN's additive row; `MENACE_WHERE_KINDS` gains `site` — MN14) | MN's commit |
| A PARAGON'S weekly choice — a post, a tower, a lair | CH §4.4's weights gain one site row (a warden weighs a watchtower, a wizard a tower, a villain an unheld keep) | CH's commit |
| THE PARTY — journey, presence, reach, clearing | PT's `presentAt` gains `siteId?` additively; `party_reached_site` (ENC-HL-04's event) and `party_made_known_site` (the party's hand on a site made known — ONE meaning, PT's kind, recorded beside the DM's own `SITE_STATUS made_known` act; PT's door for it writes no site event, so ST's closed caller list names only PT's clearing door) are PT DEED DOORS (`party_event`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 B ("the reveal" → `party_made_known_site`) · PT-r2-verify's note]; a party clearing a den is ONE act through PT's door: the `party_cleared_site` deed's door calls ST's one writer (`cleared`, holder none, `causedBy` the deed) — one act, one undo for both (CHAIR-NOTES 3: the marks "ride the act the DM already records"); a DM who records only `SITE_HOLDER` still clears the den (no deed, no renown), and ST's writer is IDEMPOTENT per (site, word, tick), so a DM who records both acts in one week writes one `cleared` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-19; PT's §4 sites bracket ("recorded beside the DM's own `SITE_HOLDER` … act") is routed for its alignment] | PT's commit |
| THE DOSSIER — the threat page and the defence page | `sitesNear` (the town's sites, their holders, their effects, each with its felt line) | ST-1 / ST-3 surfaces (EDITOR lines) |
| THE MAP — lenses and the world-site record | the record through DR-V14-1 (V08's host client; read as `WorldSite.host`, fact tier `dossier`), held sites through H13's `PolitySite` "with holder and garrison band" (`garrisonBandOf` — a READINESS word on `SITE_READINESS`, never an army band, A.10 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3]), each dated status through H9's events window — never re-derived; the map keeps its own site document joined by `mapSiteRef`; a patrol's round trip "measured from the nearest garrisoned seat or site" reads `garrisonBandOf` | ST-4; the map's M-SITE-A / M-SITE-F milestones (HR:644–647) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-7 · HOST-r2-9] |
| EVERY HEARING CARD's WHERE | the fold's WHERE table's `site` row (§3.4 companion table) | the fold |

### 7.3 THE ACTOR CONVENTION AND `site` — a WHERE and a subject, never an actor

The actor convention is SIX kinds, `settlement · person · party · menace · corps · body` (A.12, amending A.7 by RULINGS-ROUND3 §G.3 — the fold §1's requirement-24 bracket, the spine §1 mirror and the fold §3.3's row say six at P4): a corps decides (its weekly choice is its own; CH §16b) and is keyed `u:`; a menace stays an actor because the DM's authored course stands in for its mind (MN12 · MN6); a BODY decides (its weekly choice is its own, THE BODIES §4) and carries its kind as a field (`bodyKind ∈ conclave · gang · beast`, as a person's archetype is a field); A SITE HAS NO COURSE AND NO CHOICE (ST4: "A site has no mind"), so it is never an actor kind and `strategicPosture.js :: POSTURE_ACTOR_KINDS` — "THE CLOSED ACTOR SET" of posture-bearing deciders ("a posture read that quietly treats a temple as a settlement would be wrong in a way no gate could see") — gains no `site`. Both jobs a site has on a card are carried without it: the WHERE by `ENGAGEMENT_WHERE_KINDS`' `site` (the fold §3.4's companion table), the target by the `subject` dial (the proposals' `targetSaveId` stays the anchor settlement, MN9's convention). A BODY that holds, takes, besieges or clears a site is a PARTY of ST's hearings as the actor `{ kind: 'body', id }` (THE BODIES §9.4: "actor lists WIDENED"); only an id-less authored gang (before C17, §2.1), which has no record and no mind of its own, rides the `subject` dial as its holder ref until BD-0 adopts it. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.12 · BD's route (3): six kinds; a body an actor; "a band or a cult on a card rides the `subject` dial … until WC-12 mints a band actor" superseded by THE BODIES' `body`.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-14 (the RULINGS-ROUND2b ADDENDUM, "ACCEPTED as proposed", applied here and at ST18, §2.1, §7.4's SP row, §8's parties and OD-ST-27; L-ST item 20(b)'s "the actor convention gains `site`" is WITHDRAWN) · A.7 (the five kinds — ↳ r4: six, A.12) · FOLD-r2-verify's note ("ST still carries the STOP-era wording").]

| PG (pestilence) | BY THE ONE READS — the hospice's lean on `care01Of`; a plague pit's lean on the outbreak gate (one plague truth; the host's plague-10.2-7 defect is PG's) |
| the war volume | BY BOTH — `homeDefense` and the siege words; ST-2b's site target |
| the map | BY THE SITE READ — the world-site record, H13's held sites, the patrol round trip's origin, the hearing card's WHERE (channels per ST-4: DR-V14-1, H13 `PolitySite`, H9) |
| FS (THE FISCAL SEAT) [ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; `cure/D-FS-r1.md` §5 ST / PR)] | BY THE ONE READS + a slot — the `sites` upkeep row is FS's demand term (`keep_repair`) when W-COIN's coin leg lands (OD-ST-5's P6c row); nothing before it |

**Patrols considered** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 §E (one "patrols considered" line where THE PATROLS touch the volume) · RULINGS-ROUND2b HOST-r2-5]: THE PATROLS touch THE SITES at six points, each stated where it lands — the shared 0.25 suppression cap, which the keep's garrison and the town's riders feed as two distinct bodies of men (§3.3, §3.4; A.1); the one additive slot taking PR's deviation rows under one clamp (§3.3's caps); the watchtower as an input of PR's `witnessedCrossing` (ST17); a grouped patrol as a clearer of dens (§6.1 step 6); PR's pay gate on `settlementFundingBand` (§2.4; the one name, A.9); and the keep that the levy never draws (§4.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 · PR-V2 (A.1) · note 5]. The host's patrol facts go OUT through the events window (H9) like every other fact; H16 is the map's inbound door, which the host never consumes.
