[REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's `site_fallen` (CLIMAX; `holds · falls`) and `site_siege` (STANDING; the siege words) were one fact heard twice; ONE kind, `site_siege`, carries it on the registry's one siege vocabulary.] **No party kind:** none of ST's kinds is a party kind; the party's dealings with a site are PT's doors, and requirement 24's party default ("nothing has happened to the party yet") is never ST's to compute. **Not hearings:** the party's reach (`party_reached_site`) and its hand on a site made known (`party_made_known_site`) — PT deed doors, never the engine deciding the party's fate [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — "the reveal" → PT's kind]; an authored force holder (the DM's act is the verdict; it is told, §6.2 — save at a kept-secret site, A.16); a body's own leaving or end (its course, receipted and told, §6.1 step 1); wear, restoration and abandonment (the world's slow course, receipted, never a docket item — they decide nothing between two parties); the DM's own verbs. **The three choices** (requirement 24): at a one-week advance the DM may leave the week to the engine, tilt it, or give the verdict; the hearing seal is read FIRST by ST's resolvers. **Only on the week:** each kind is minted only at the DM's one-week advance and lives that week; a month, season or year advance resolves every week on the default and leaves each week's receipt; nothing queues (requirement 24's binding on every kind). **The docket:** ST registers its FIVE resolvers with SP-9 (`registerEngagementKind`; `site_sale` added in round 4, L-BD 30); with `hearingWeekEnabled` dark the default resolves in its tick. **One contest per site per week, one side at a time** (A.18 · A.3; §6.1 step 6d): only the ANCHOR'S SIDE joins — its courts (the anchor leading, else the nearest), their patrols, a body the leader harbours or employs; a hostile body, a menace or an army moving on the same site that week is its own CHALLENGER side — the strongest contests the holder (or takes the empty site), every other waits at the approach and, if it still moves on the site, arrives and contests next week; ONE hearing per ARRIVAL, ONE contest per site per week; the leader of the side that wins a `site_cleared` holds the site after, the joiners receipted; with the pause dark the week's contests are drawn by ST's closing call after THE BODIES' leaf — never two contests and never a second card for the same fact [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3 · ST-X1: round 4's line summed "whatever courts, patrols or bodies" into one force]. **The card** (requirement 24(g), the Evidence Law): who, upon whom, where, the default — "Brigands under the Red Hand have come to the abandoned keep at Thornwatch. Left alone, they hold it." · "Kell's levy has marched on the robbers at the Crossed Keys. Left alone, the levy drives them out." · "The keep at Thornwatch, Kell's last, stands empty. Kell may send a garrison." · "Ashford asks the Grey Lady's flock to give up the watchtower above the town for grain. Left alone, the flock yields." (`site_sale`, L-BD 30) · "Kell and Tolle have ridden out together against the robbers at the ford." (one contest, A.18) · "The Ashen Host has come to the keep at Thornwatch, which the Red Hand holds; the Hollow Road gang waits below it." (one side at a time, A.3) · "Ashford's levy marches to take the watchtower from the Grey Lady's flock, which refused its grain." (the seizure, `site_cleared`, A.1) On scrutiny the card names its READ (the force's band against the holder's might, in the might words; against a tied garrison, its readiness word — "Kell's keep at Thornwatch: manned" — never an army word, A.10). **RN:** a court's clearing mints no deed until RN's ENGINE-SOURCE slot opens; ST names its candidate source row `site_verdict` for that slot (RN §3.1), its kinds the five (with `site_sale` — ROUTED to RN, V1 note 4); ONE HUNT, ONE DEED (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5 — the hearing's owner mints): a site contest won by a LED hunt (a paragon or the public-security office-holder the card names at the head, CH's `hunt_body` aim) is CH's `hunt_won` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify note 2] at the mission's close, doer the leader, and no `site_verdict` is minted beside it; an unled court's clearing is the court's tale (RN1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5].

| Flag | Gates | Requires | Lit-mutant pins | Presets it may light in |

| `siteEffectsEnabled` | every lean of §3 (`siteTermAt`, `siteDefense`, the presence feeder and suppressor, the watchtower's input of `witnessedCrossing`, the `scoreRoute` term); lit, it also lights the ONE presence composer — A.3's shared car lights when ANY registered feeder's own flag is lit, so ST's danger arm never waits on `menacesEnabled`; a body-held site's presence and raider-gate rows yield to THE BODIES' where `bodyEffectsEnabled` is lit (§3.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — MN-V6 (A.3) · note 5] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (2)] | `worldSitesEnabled` | dark: every host's lean returns its neutral (×1 rows absent, +0) and `homeDefense` is byte-identical; lit-mutant: a sound funded keep at hand moves `homeDefense` by its term; an untied keep's town latches embattled on a canonized map | the stressor presets (gates, counterforces, care, faith arms by their own flags); the danger arm on canonized worlds; the defence arm under `warLayerEnabled` |

| `siteWarTargetEnabled` | ST-2b: a settlement's army may target a site; the approach fort's `site_siege` on PS's transit leaf | `siteFatesEnabled` ∧ `warLayerEnabled` | dark: a deployment carrying `siteTargetId` WITHDRAWS through the landed withdrawal path (one dark-after-lit path, §11 B), never `continue`; a world with none is byte-identical [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-27] | the war presets |

## §10 THE WAVES (each carries requirement 16's FIRST-VISIBLE, 17's TIER and DEGRADED ARM, L10's five-member EDITOR line, 13's Alignment line and 20's `causedBy`)

ST is a coupling CARRIER across **WAR · TRADE · INFO · FAITH · HEALTH**; its prefix `ST` is chartered with its first coupling-registry row (requirement 21(a)/(c)). HBF rows minted, each in its wave's commit (L10(a)): `siteTakenContest`, `siteClearedContest`, `siteSiegeVerdict` (each one keyed draw; `siteTakenContest` added in round 2 with `site_taken`'s folded sortie, ST-r2-17). Chain **C15 THE SITES** sits with C14 THE PASSAGE (they share the border gate), after C0 · C0.5 · C1 and before the polity's deeper waves (the chair's order note, ruling 22); its FIRST FELT CHAIN is ST-0 → ST-1 (the fortification alone); every other kind lights with its own system (§5).

**ST-0 — THE RECORD, THE DM'S HAND, THE MAP'S ADOPTION.**
- **Car-0 (a MEASUREMENT before any ST line):** re-measure MN-0's stage host with ST's leaf beside MN's — and ST-2's closing call after THE BODIES' leaf (+1, §6.1 step 6d) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3] — (`calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` 461; MN-0's four STOP conditions inherited verbatim; ⛔ STOP at build if the host cannot carry a second leaf under 800 or if MN-0 chose another host — then ST's stage follows MN's host); measure `realmManifest.js` (474) and `realmVerbExecution.js` (663) for five entries (leaf-first: the entry objects live in `sites/siteVerbs.js`, the manifest spreads them, ≤ 3 host lines; the apply arm dispatches to `sites/siteVerbApply.js`, ≤ 4 host lines); and NAME THE RUIN CARD'S HOST from §6.5's two candidates (`components/map/HeraldRemembrance.jsx`, 255 physical lines, recommended; or the `FORCE_ABANDON` approval card) by its line budget — one link, a UI leaf [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-64)].
- **Leaves.** `src/domain/sites/siteLedger.js` (the one writer, `applySiteEvent`; the normalizer; FNV ids; the vocabularies of §2.3); `sites/siteRead.js` (`siteAt`, `sitesNear`, `holderSinceOf` [P5 — WC-ST-93], `attachmentsOf`, `fundingBandOf`, `siteReadinessOf`, `garrisonBandOf`, `believedDenOf`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-V3 · ST-V1: the readiness read and the belief read named on their leaf]; `sites/siteStage.js :: advanceSites` (steps 1–3 of §6.1); `sites/siteVerbs.js` + `siteVerbApply.js`. **Host edits.** `calamityKernel.js` (+3); `realmManifest.js` (+≤3); `realmVerbExecution.js` (+≤4); `spatialUsage.js` (a TRACKED `worldSites` row); the dying settlement's card (the "leave a ruin" offer; a UI leaf).

## §11 THE LIFECYCLE OF EVERY PERSISTED SHAPE (requirement 22a; a requirement-23 horizon for each)

**A. `spatialLedgers.worldSites` (the record, with its `events` ring, `lastHolder`, `siege`, `fundingSet`, round 2's `wantSince`, `mapSiteRef`, `keptSecret`, and round 4's body refs and an id-less gang's `holder.might`; round 3's `holder.seenBy` WITHDRAWN in round 5 — ST stores no belief, A.5).** [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5]

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | only the DM (`PLACE_SITE`; the map's adoption; "leave a ruin"); the engine never spawns one (ST3) |
| read | `siteRead.js` and `siteEffects.js` only; the window; the dossier |
| persist | `spatialLedgers.worldSites`, drop-when-empty at every optional key and at the ledger (the namespace drops with its last sub-ledger); a TRACKED row in `spatialUsage.js` |
| regenerate | a regenerated SETTLEMENT does not touch the site; if the regeneration removes the anchor or holder, §6.1 step 1 applies (ST14); a regenerated WORLD (a new seed) carries no sites (THE PROMISE: sites are never generated) |
| undo | the advance ring's snapshot undo restores the ledger whole (the landed undo of `worldState`); a DM verb's undo is its proposal's undo |
| import | an imported world without a site's anchor: the site stands, unheld and re-attached (ST14); an imported site whose kind or band is not in the closed lists is REFUSED with a typed receipt (finite semantics), never coerced |
| migrate | none at landing (a new sub-ledger); the `where.position` arm is additive when the canon carries points (H5); a rename of any vocabulary withdraws pending decrees through §20.3's resolver; the compile's `hidden` / `revealed` were renamed before any build (`keptSecret` / `made_known`), so nothing migrates |
| dark-after-lit | the ledger is KEPT and inert (no lean, no stage step); relighting resumes from it, dropping every `wantSince` at relighting's first tick so no dark week counts toward want (step 3 re-arms it); the verbs vanish from the manifest. THE BODIES dark-after-lit (ST lit): a body holder's ref STAYS; its presence and raider-gate rows read on the body RECORD's might as any untied holder's (§2.4, §3.3 — BD §15's "ST's holder refs read as untied holders with the record's `might`"), a conclave's site reads as any faith holder of its creed, every body holder's band reads `unfunded`, the sale and the stance gate are absent (every force arriver hostile, as before) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (2)] |
| join (the map) | `mapSiteRef` carried at adoption; at a founding over a site the map retires its document and this record keeps the id; at that town's death the map is asked to re-emit it under the same id (§6.5); a `SITE_KIND` act re-kinds both, keeping the id [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-8] |
| adoption (THE BODIES, BD-0) | when `bodiesEnabled` first lights on a world with id-less authored gangs, BD-0 mints one gang record per such holder (`den_held`, its might the DM's `holder.might`) and ST's one writer fills `holder.ref.id` and DROPS `holder.might` — the same holder (no holder change, no clock dropped — §6.1 step 3's carve-out), one might from then (the record's); ST hands NO sightings over (it holds none, A.5): THE BODIES' belief record of the adopted gang begins from the tellings each court holds (ROUTE BD) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (4)] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5 · V2 note 1] |
| promotion (A.2) | the DM's `PROMOTE_BODY` → `RAISE_MENACE`, approved: in the same act THE BODIES' ledger writes the body's `raised` end and calls ST's one writer, which RE-POINTS the holder ref to `{ kind: 'menace', id }` with ONE `raised` event — no `cleared`, no unmaking telling, no reclaim offer, no sighting written by ST; a holder change by the full identity (`fundingSet` drops; the menace's band `unfunded`); undo is the proposal ring's snapshot restore (the record, the body and the menace restore together — MN §2); dark (THE BODIES or the menace volume dark, or OD-BD-6 unanswered) the verb does not exist and nothing happens [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2 · RU-MN-r4] |
| holder change (`wantSince`, `fundingSet`; `holder.seenBy` withdrawn in round 5 — the sightings live in MN's one store, B.2) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — ST-r5-verify A.5 residue: the §11 label] | the one writer compares the holder's FULL IDENTITY `{ class, ref.kind, ref.bodyKind, ref.id, role, creed }` before and after EVERY event and, whenever any part differs (`placed · garrisoned · taken · reclaimed · conveyed · ceded · granted · seized · consecrated · sold · cleared · abandoned`, a body's own take or leaving, BD-0's adoption NOT included — filling an id-less gang's `id` keeps the holder), DROPS `wantSince` AND `fundingSet` (the want and the DM's ceiling are always the current holder's; step 3 re-arms the want on the next unfunded read; a body holder sets none) and replaces the whole `holder` object — which carries no sighting (A.5): the courts' beliefs of the old holder stand in its owner's record, stale, until a newer sighting or telling; an event that installs a FORCE holder (an arrival's `taken`, an authored placement, a `site_siege` fall, a body's take, a won `site_cleared`'s new holder, `made_known` of a held site) RETURNS the at-hand courts' sightings `{ courtId, holderRef, siteId, mightBand, tick }` for MN's one door `recordSighting` (B.2) — none at a `keptSecret` site — and a contest a court fights at the site WRITES OR REFRESHES that court's row there too (a patrol's look writes its own, PR's door) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — ST-r2-16 (the §11 row RULINGS-ROUND3 B requires) · ST-V1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.20 · ST-r2-16 (a)(b) · A.17 · ST-W2 · A.16] |
| `holderSince` (P5) | CREATE · UPDATE only by the one writer at a holder change (the same full-identity comparison); DROPPED when the site falls unheld; READ only by `holderSinceOf` (THE BODIES' tenure read); PERSIST drop-when-absent; UNDO the ring's snapshot; IMPORT a value after the record's `lastTick` or on an unheld site is dropped (re-set at the next holder change); MIGRATE none (nothing built); DARK-AFTER-LIT kept, inert; a relit world's first holder change re-sets it [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-17 (WC-ST-93)] |
| death | a holder's or anchor's death → §6.1 step 1; a menace holder's end → `cleared`; a BODY holder's end (THE BODIES' `ended` row) → `cleared`, its leaving → `abandoned`, each with its unmaking telling (none at a `keptSecret` site) — save a `raised` end, which re-points the site to the menace (the promotion row above); a person holder only by the DM's verdict [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route · note 2] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2] |
| veil | a `keptSecret` site emits NO telling — arrival, authored placement, siege fall or unmaking — and occasions no sighting until `made_known` (A.16); until then a court knows it only by WITNESS (a patrol's look, the warden, the holder's act seen on its land), written into the holder's one belief for that court alone — in the omniscient arm too, where the truth arm knows no kept-secret site but the witness informs the court that saw (V2 note 6) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — V2 note 6 · A.5]; `den_unanswered` never opens for it; NO site is on a player-facing surface except through the map's reveal list (the hard-deny holds `'spatialLedgers'`); the DM sees it always [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-19 · HOST-r2-7 (4)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.16 · ST-W3: round 3's "or until its holder acts where it is felt" lifted the veil for every court; now a witness informs only the court that saw] |
| horizon (requirement 23) | the `events` ring keeps the newest 8 per site (DRAFT); older events live in the chronicle (the advance history); the ledger is bounded by the DM's placements (no engine growth) |

**B. `deployments[*].siteTargetId` (ST-2b; the war's record, a declared additive field).** create — the war chooser's site target; read — the siege loop; persist — the war's `deployments` (drop-when-absent); regenerate — none; undo — the ring; import — refused if the site is unknown (the deployment resolves as a withdrawal, the landed wind-down); migrate — none; dark-after-lit — `siteWarTargetEnabled` dark: the deployment withdraws (the landed withdrawal path), never `continue`s silently; death — the site ended → the deployment withdraws; veil — none. Horizon: the deployment's own life. Declared as OD-ST-33, with a row owed to the fold §7's shape list (routed) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-27].

**B′. `deployments[*].keepBreaches?` (CONDITIONAL — exists ONLY if ST-1's car-0 STOP fires, §4.2; the war's record, a declared additive field on a LANDED record — ⭐ the owner's shape, OD-ST-53).** create — the war layer's keep leaf, the tick a rolled fall lands on a qualifying keep (a count); read — ST's stage the same tick, which applies the breach step idempotent by (deployment, count); persist — the war's `deployments`, drop-when-absent; regenerate — none; undo — the advance ring (the deployment and the site restored by one snapshot, so a re-run tick re-stamps and re-applies once); import — a non-integer or a count on a deployment with no site target at a keep is dropped; migrate — none (nothing built); dark-after-lit — `siteEffectsEnabled` dark: the leaf stamps nothing and the stage applies nothing; a stamped count is inert until relit and then applied once (idempotent) — the count SURVIVES a dark world's loads because master's `worldPulse/worldState.js :: normalizeDeployments` is UNGATED and "retain[s] every deployment field" (CONFIRMED at `81f1cd963`); the import drop above is ONE fail-closed block added there beside `blocks`'s (the same ungated persistence-hygiene law, never flag-gated), counted in ST-1's host edits [REFINED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — measured after V-ALL-p5's P5-V1 (a dark normalizer DELETING rows another volume called "kept, inert"): this shape is not exposed to it; the import arm's host named]; death — dies with the deployment; veil — none (a breach is the siege's own fact). Horizon: the deployment's own life [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-12 (WC-ST-86)].

## §14 WHAT A DM READS (the Evidence Law: who, which power, what effect; deeds only, never forecasts; no numerals; no mechanism names)

1. "Kell's keep stands ready; Ashford's host is before it." — the siege card (surface); on scrutiny the card names the keep's term in the defence read. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-20: "cannot easily take the town while it holds" was a forecast.]
2. "Kell's keep is breached; the town's walls still hold." — the siege card and the Herald (surface).
3. "Brigands hold the old keep above the road, and Kell's roads are dangerous." — the dossier's threat page (surface); the danger lens behind its toggle (scrutiny). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — ST-r2-20: "Tolle's are watchful" asserted nothing a read had done; Tolle's line, where its row fires, is the gate row's own reason.]
4. "The keep at Thornwatch has stood empty since Kell stopped paying its garrison." — the dossier (surface); the dated events (scrutiny).
5. "Kell let its border keeps fall empty, and robbers have taken two of them; Kell's lord has not come to clear them." — the Herald when a den presses a sworn member a season unanswered (surface); PL's `protection_failed` on the sworn edge, which the crown's hold reads (scrutiny). Its mirror, when the liege answers inside the season: "Kell's lord rode out and cleared the robbers from Tolle's border keep." — PL's `protection_kept` on the same record [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.6] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.6: the row moves the hold's `P`; "and the commons' grievance" named no read the row moves] — ruling 12's legible consequence: a crown that lets its border forts rot loses their protection AND makes dens that press its own members.
6. "Kell's levy drove the robbers out of the Crossed Keys." — the Herald (surface).
7. "The warded tower above Kell holds the dead ground back." — the deadzone's resolution receipt (scrutiny).
8. "The abbey at Merewick keeps the old faith strong in the valley." — the faith page (scrutiny).
9. "The hospice at Tolle took in the sick." — the plague's course (surface in an outbreak).
10. "Thornwatch's watchers saw the column cross the ford." — the belief presets' news (surface).
11. "Kell is gone, but its keep still stands over the empty valley." — the chronicle (surface) when a settlement founded over a site fails.
12. "The Ashen Host has made the old keep at Thornwatch its lair." — the Herald (surface) when the DM's menace takes a site.
13. "The Hill Wolves keep their camp in the old ruin above Tolle." — the dossier (surface) the week the DM raises the gang that held it into a menace: the same place, a new name, no clearing (A.2) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.2].
14. "Ashford's levy took the watchtower from the Grey Lady's flock, which had refused its grain." — the Herald (surface) after a seizure by a curbing court; on scrutiny the refused demand and the stance that hardened to outlawing (A.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.1].
15. "The Hollow Road gang waits below Thornwatch while the Ashen Host holds the keep." — the dossier (surface) when a weaker side holds at the approach that week (A.3) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.3].

The surface shows the site's holder, its condition word and its one felt line behind the map's site toggle and on the dossier; the funding band, the distance bands and each lean's term are scrutiny only (the Why panel, the cause walk); the machinery (the factors, the caps) is never pushed.
